## astral_rift.1000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在裂隙的另一边有什么东西控制住了球体使我们的回收装置失效了。\n\n我们收到的第一批数据难以理解，但随后的传输让这些信息慢慢拼凑到了一起。我们推测，在这条裂缝的另一边，时间的流动要快得多，事实上，我们正在接收来自裂缝另一边的实时通信。\n\n结果加是每天处理大约一个单词。以下是我们目前收到的报告。\n\n§E“我们观测……”§!\n\n§E“道歉……方法……恶劣……”§!\n\n§E“紧急……”§!\n\n§E“理解……”§!\n\n这些简短的信息几被严重的噪音和无法解读的数据几乎淹没了。\n\n谁都不知道§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的[Explorer.GetCrew]究竟处于何等的危险之中。\n\n可能可以通过锚索发送电磁脉冲来重新建立联系并重新掌控我们的控制系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:7`

### English

Something on the far side of the Rift has taken hold of the Sphere and disabled our retraction device.\n\nThe first parcels of data we received were unintelligible, but subsequent transfers are slowly piecing these messages together. We speculate the flow of time is much faster on the other side of this rift, and we are in fact receiving communication in real-time from across the breach.\n\nThe result is the processing of approximately one word per day. The following report is what we have received so far.\n\n§E"we observe..."§!\n\n§E"apology... method... unkind..."§!\n\n§E"emergency..."§!\n\n§E"understand..."§!\n\nThese brief messages are hidden amongst heavy noise and incomprehensible data.\n\nWho knows what danger the [Explorer.GetCrew] of the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! is in.\n\nIt may be possible to re-establish contact and regain control by sending an electromagnetic pulse through the anchor cord in an effort to reset our control systems.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:9`

## astral_rift.1005.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1005.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

这些图像中可以拼凑出我们整个星系的有赢，还有另一个小得多、但密度异常大的星系的图像。在两者之间，有一系列的星界裂隙。我们明白这是从我们的维度到另一个地方的路线图。\n\n在我们星系的图像中，有一个小光点异常地突出。后来的分析证实这是我们的家园，[Explorer.GetHomeWorldName]，在闪闪发光。在邻近的宇宙中也发现了类似的光。\n\n他们在告诉我们该去哪里。\n\n为了避免数据损坏或丢失，我们接收的数据已经超出了计算机的合理处理能力。我们应该优先考虑我们想要保存的信息类型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

A projection of our galaxy could be pieced together from the images, along with a second image of a much smaller, yet unusually dense system. Between the two, there stretched a series of Astral Rifts. We understood that this was a roadmap from our dimension to somewhere else.\n\nA small point of light was abnormally highlighted inside the image of our galaxy. Later analysis confirmed this was our Homeworld, [Explorer.GetHomeWorldName], shining brightly. A similar light was discovered to be marked in the neighboring universe.\n\nThey are showing us where to go.\n\nWe're receiving more data than our computers can reasonably handle before it becomes corrupted or lost. We should prioritize the type of information we'd like to preserve.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:16`

## astral_rift.1005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能破译出的后两个单词是§E“家园”§!和§E“目击”§!。令人惊讶的是，随着我们对这些怪异数据理解的深入，我们还发现了嵌入在噪音中的视觉数据的存在。\n\n$astral_rift.1005.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:14`

### English

The next two words we were able to decode were §E"home"§! and §E"witness"§!. Amazingly, as our understanding of the alien data improves, we also discovered the existence of visual data embedded within the noise.\n\n$astral_rift.1005.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:17`

## astral_rift.1006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在额外的计算支持下，我们处理了他们传输回的数据。这些数据其实是通讯，不是任何的攻击或什么别的。我们收到的数据是以一个运动图像序列讲述的故事。\n\n$astral_rift.1005.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:18`

### English

With the extra computational support, we have processed the data they are streaming to us. It is in fact a communication; it was not an attack of any kind. The data we are receiving tells us a story in a sequence of moving images.\n\n$astral_rift.1005.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:22`

## astral_rift.1010.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1010.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们通过锚链传输的电磁脉冲成功地让球体从束缚它的东西中挣脱出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:22`

### English

The E.M.P. we delivered through the anchor cord successfully jolted the Sphere free of whatever had taken control over it.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:27`

## astral_rift.1010.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1010.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器完全恢复运转，船员在他们的位置附近观察到一个气态实体；可能这就是它控制了这艘船。似乎电磁冲击使它丧失了能力，至少暂时是这样。\n\n球体被固定在某种口袋维度中。我们被密集星云的旋涡云所包围。唯一检测到的其他实体就是附近的这个生物。\n\n从震动中恢复过来后，它开始用一系列快速通信和信号轰炸球体。\n\n我们的电脑和传感器信息过载，导航变得困难了起来。我们需要一些计算支持来理解我们接收到的信息。\n\n我们尚不清楚在这里到底是不是受到了欢迎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

With sensors fully operational, the crew observed a gaseous entity directly nearby their location; presumably this is what had been controlling the vessel. It seemed that the jolt of energy has incapacitated it, at least for the time being.\n\nThe Sphere is being held in some sort of pocket dimension. We are surrounded on all sides by the swirling clouds of a dense nebula. The only other entity detected is the creature nearby.\n\nAfter recovering from the shock, it has begun to bombard the Sphere with a series of rapid communications and signals.\n\nOur computers and sensors have become overloaded with information, making navigation difficult. We will need some computational support to understand what we are receiving.\n\nIt is not clear that we are welcome here.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:28`

## astral_rift.1015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

更多的图像。\n\n我们非常详细地看到了这个外星家园的编年史。\n\n这些生物是几千年前由聚集在一个巨大的星界裂隙附近的气体和能量的偶然结合而形成的。他们能像大气层的一部分般自由地进出他们的星球。\n\n在每个生命周期结束时，它们的精华会分散到行星上，重新诞生。天空中的城市崛起，陨落，又复兴。文明是在先前文明的基础上形成的，其知识是由这些祖先粒子在物理上维持的。时间流逝的程度被可靠地估计为比我们已知宇宙的历史还要大的数量级。\n\n最后，我们看到了一个觉醒的高等生物的文明。它们的生理机能比我们能想到的任何技术都要先进。\n\n我们在数据库中记录了这个新物种，命名为无形者。\n\n§E“繁荣……”§!\n\n再一次，我们接收的数据比我们的计算机在损坏或丢失之前能够合理处理的数据还要多。我们应该优先考虑我们想要保存的信息类型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:29`

### English

More images.\n\nA chronicle of this alien homeworld was shown to us in great detail.\n\nThese beings formed millennia ago by a chance combination of gases and energy that accumulated nearby a massive Astral Rift. They flow freely in and out of their planet as if they were part of its atmosphere.\n\nAt the end of each life cycle, their essence disperses back into the planet and gets born anew. Cities in the sky rose, fell, and rose again. Civilizations formed on top of previous civilizations with the knowledge physically maintained by these ancestral particles. The degree of the time passing was safely estimated to be magnitudes greater than the history of our known universe.\n\nIn the end, we behold a civilization of awakened higher beings. Their physiology alone is more advanced than any technology we could conceive.\n\nWe have logged this new species in our databases as the Formless.\n\n§E"flourishing..."§!\n\nOnce again, we're receiving more data than our computers can reasonably handle before it becomes corrupted or lost. We should prioritize the type of information we'd like to preserve.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:35`

## astral_rift.1020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

它们的气态母行星向我们展示了越来越多的细节。这一次，我们看到了它的个体居民。光、气体和星界物质构成的存在从附近的裂隙泄漏出来，生活在一个可谓是乌托邦的地方。\n\n最终，一座伟大的建筑建成了。这项令人难以置信的工程直接从他们的星球延伸到裂隙，使他们能够轻松地在这个维度和无数其他维度之间旅行。在这两个位面之间有这样一条星界管路存在了这么长时间，他们塑造了许多裂隙来满足他们的特殊需求。他们的社会在多个维度之间延伸，殖民整个宇宙，就像我们殖民新行星一样。\n\n然而，他们最大的成就也是他们最大的灾难。他们的帝国扩张得比星界管路所能维持的还要远。这个巨型结构越来越亮，直到目不能视，最终在一次跨越维度的灾难中猛烈地爆炸，彻底摧毁了他们的文明。\n\n§E“破碎的亲缘……”§!\n\n§E“战争……”§!\n\n§E”帮助我们……”§!\n\n我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!被强行拉出裂谷。一个维度传送门在[solar_system.GetName]恒星系中徐徐打开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

Their gaseous homeworld was revealed to us in more and more detail. This time, we see its individual inhabitants. Beings of light, gas, and astral material leaked from the nearby rift are living in what could easily be described as a utopia.\n\nEventually, a great structure was raised. An incredible piece of engineering, it stretched directly from their planet into the rift, allowing them to easily travel between this dimension and countless others. With such an astral conduit existing for so long between the planes, they have shaped many rifts to fit their specific needs. Their society stretched between a multitude of dimensions, colonizing entire universes as we would new planets.\n\nHowever, their greatest achievement was also their greatest disaster. Their empire spread further than the astral conduit could maintain. Brighter and brighter the megastructure grew until the sight of it was difficult to look upon without shielding one's eyes, until it finally burst violently in a dimension-spanning explosion that fractured their civilization.\n\n§E"shattered kin..."§!\n\n§E"war..."§!\n\n§E"help us..."§!\n\nOur §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! has been forcibly ejected from the Rift. A dimensional portal is now opening in the [solar_system.GetName] System.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:40`

## astral_rift.1020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

Escape

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:44`

## astral_rift.1025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

系统重新上线后，我们可以拉回锚链，从那个晶体裂隙里的生物处逃离。\n\n我们不知道他们还会对我们施加什么进一步的操纵力量，但最好还是不要知道的好。\n\n幸运的是，它在我们的船体上留下的残留物是极其密集的星界物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:37`

### English

With our systems re-enabled, we've retracted the anchor cable and escaped from the creature inside of the Crystal Rift.\n\nIt is unknown what further powers of manipulation they would have deployed on us, and it is best not to find out.\n\nOn a fortunate note, the residue it left on the hull of our vessel is extremely dense with astral material.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:45`

## astral_rift.1030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

封印

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:38`

### English

The Seal

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:47`

## astral_rift.1030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个最近在我们首都星系构建起来的星门将我们的人民置于高度的危险之中。\n\n幸运的是，我们通过研究星界裂隙得到的科技可以用来永远的封上这个危险。\n\n究竟是什么东西想从这里出来仍是个谜。\n\n我们的人民认为这是对一个深不可测的强大敌人的伟大胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

A gateway was nearly established in our capital system that placed our people in great danger.\n\nLuckily, with technology we have gained from studying Astral Rifts, we were able to successfully seal this danger permanently.\n\nWhatever was seeking to come through will remain a mystery.\n\nOur people consider this a great victory over an unfathomably powerful enemy.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:48`

## astral_rift.1100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巨卵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:45`

### English

Giant Eggs

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:57`

## astral_rift.1100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的探测器发现了一窝巨大的的蛋，每一颗都有§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!那么大。这些蛋预计很快就要孵化，这样一来这种我们连其近亲是什么样都不了解的生物很快就要慢行星都是了。我们探测不到这颗行星上生活着其他生物，所以该物种的生长周期也扑朔迷离。\n\n在这颗几乎荒芜的行星上，生态系统并不像能维持这么庞大种群数量的样子。对此我们的主要猜想是该物种遵循着某种星际迁徙模式。因为该物种的成体目前必然是在星球之外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:46`

### English

Our probe has discovered a clutch of giant eggs, each as big as the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!. They are expected to hatch imminently, and when they do the planet will be crawling with creatures we know next to nothing about. We can detect no other living specimens on the planet, so the growth cycle of this species is unknown to us.\n\nMostly barren, this planet's ecosystem does not seem capable of supporting a thriving population. Our leading theory is that this species follows an interstellar migration pattern. The adults of the species must therefore currently be off-world.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:58`

## astral_rift.1105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

饮星者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

Star Drinkers

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:60`

## astral_rift.1105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

蛋正在孵化。\n\n巨大的有翼生物纷纷破壳而出。它们生下来便具有飞行能力，一大群一大群地在空中畅游，巨大的翅膀在大陆上投下片片阴影。他们在大气层中扶摇直上，似乎正在追逐着星光。\n\n我们还未观测到它们的进食行为，着可能表明它们是以某种形式的光合作用获取的能量。它们的生理学特性带来了许多疑问。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

The eggs are hatching.\n\nMassive, winged creatures are emerging from the shells. They are born with the ability to fly, swarming the skies in huge flocks, their enormous wings casting shadows across the landscape. They climb high into the atmosphere, seemingly attracted to the starlight.\n\nWe have yet to observe them feed, which could indicate that they draw energy via some form of photosynthesis. Their physiology raises many questions.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:61`

## astral_rift.1105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1105.a&lang=en)

靠近点。

### English

Get closer.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1105.b&lang=en)

### English

## astral_rift.1110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

友好的幼雏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

Friendly Hatchlings

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:65`

## astral_rift.1110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们靠近时这些生物没有感到害怕，也许是在他们孤立的栖息地没有什么猎食者的缘故。\n\n他们好奇地围绕着我们飞行，因此我们很容易从这些生物和卵的残余物中取样。它们长有光伏鳞片，可以从恒星中获得所有必要的给养。它们的身体也具有很强的抗压性，表明它们能够长时间抵抗宇宙中的真空环境。这些生物很有可能已经进化到能够进行长时间的太空飞行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

The creatures showed no fear as we approached them, probably due to a lack of predators within their isolated habitat.\n\nWe were able to easily take samples from the beings and the remains of their eggs as they flew curiously around us. They have photovoltaic scales, allowing them to gain all necessary sustenance from their star. Their bodies are also highly resistant to pressure, indicating an ability to withstand the vacuum of space for long periods of time. It is highly likely that these creatures have evolved to be capable of prolonged space flight.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:66`

## astral_rift.1110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1110.a&lang=en)

### English

## astral_rift.1115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恒星帆航

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

Solar Sailing

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:71`

## astral_rift.1115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

观察这些幼体的初次飞行很有启发性。\n\n它们像恒星帆一样拍打着翅膀，熟练地加速到非常快，在上层大气中嬉戏打闹时不断来回滑翔俯冲。\n\n他们不受重力束缚，能像爬到上层大气一般跨越宇宙空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

Observing the first flight of these infant creatures is illuminating.\n\nThey flap their wings like solar sails, masterfully accelerating to great speeds, gliding and swooping as they play together in the upper atmosphere.\n\nThey are unbound by gravity, capable of traversing space in the same way they effortlessly climb through the atmosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:72`

## astral_rift.1115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

印象深刻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

Impressive.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:73`

## astral_rift.1120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星界迁徙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

Astral Migration

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:75`

## astral_rift.1120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对这些幼雏的行为模式进行进一步研究的过程中，一只比战列舰还大的个体倏然而至。那些生物纷纷围绕了过去，争抢地想要接近这只巨兽。似乎这就是同一物种的一只成体。\n\n这颗隐蔽的星球看来是这些跨次元生物的安全筑巢区。在我们的注视下，那只成体赶着幼雏朝上层大气中新形成的裂缝飞去。\n\n它们穿过裂缝时留下了一道空间扰动的涟漪，以及大量的$astral_threads$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:60`

### English

While conducting further research into the behavioral patterns of the hatchlings, a being larger than a battleship suddenly phased into existence. The creatures flocked around it, tussling among themselves to get closer to the giant. It appears to be a mature member of the same species.\n\nThis secluded planet must therefore serve as a safe nesting area for these trans-dimensional beings. As we watched, the adult herded the young toward a newly formed rift in the upper atmosphere.\n\nTheir passage left a rippling wake of spatial disruption, as well as a large concentration of $astral_threads$.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:76`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1120.a&lang=en)

## astral_rift.1200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

加速度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

Acceleration

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:90`

## astral_rift.1200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在球体穿过裂隙时，我们经历了一阵剧烈的加速。\n\n这个宇宙的巨大引力把我们拉了进去。在经历了一段不舒服又危险的旅程后，我们发现自己漂浮在一个密集的星系群中，每一个星系都在我们球体有限的传感器范围内。据我们所知，这似乎构成了整个宇宙。\n\n初步报告还指出这里的暗物质的含量很高；更重要的是，质量还在不断增加。直接的结果是，随着时间的推移，引力正在增强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

As our Sphere passed through the rift, we experienced a wild burst of acceleration.\n\nThe tremendous gravity of this universe was pulling us in. After an uncomfortable and dangerous ride, we found ourselves floating within a dense cluster of star systems, every one of which was within range of our Sphere's limited sensors. As far as we can tell, these comprise the totality of this universe.\n\nInitial reports also indicate high levels of dark matter; what's more, this mass is constantly increasing. As a direct consequence, the forces of gravity are intensifying over time.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:91`

## astral_rift.1205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

皱缩空间

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

Shrinking Space

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:93`

## astral_rift.1205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个宇宙的尺寸正在皱缩，我们预测它会在某一时刻将完全坍缩。\n\n问题是我们无法准确地计算出这种情况何时会发生。自从我们到达之后，球体的系统一直表现得很奇怪，计算机核心上的许多数据点都消失了，如同从来没有被写入过一般。\n\n在其他地方，人们观察到一株盆栽植物收起了根，又变成了种子。时间在这里显然是不稳定的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

This universe is shrinking in size. In due time, we predict it will completely collapse into itself.\n\nThe problem is that we cannot accurately calculate when this will occur. The Sphere's systems have been acting strangely since we arrived, and various data points have disappeared from the computer core. It is as if the data itself has been unwritten.\n\nElsewhere, a potted plant was observed lifting its roots and returning to a seed. The flow of time is clearly unstable here.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:94`

## astral_rift.1210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

时间泄露

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

Temporal Leak

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:96`

## astral_rift.1210.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1210.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在球体之外，后果更加戏剧性：小行星的轨道完全颠倒了，行星分散成尘埃和气体，整个星系都反向凝结成挥发性物质的球。时间显然是反向流动的，而我们被夹在两者之间的某个地方。\n\n最终，时间将达到“零点”，这个宇宙将缩小到一个针尖大小。\n\n虽然我们似乎与时间效应有些隔离，但球体显然不是密不透风的。通过让我们的电脑和裂隙外的[Fleet.GetName]同步，我们应该能够保存那些迅速消失的数据。作为一个更激进的措施，我们可以使用星界丝缕的独特属性来重新极化船体并密封时间漏洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

Outside of the Sphere the consequences are even more dramatic: the trajectory of asteroids have completely reversed, planets are dispersing into dust and gas, entire systems are condensing into balls of volatile material. Time is clearly flowing backward, and we are caught somewhere in between.\n\nEventually, the time will reach "zero" and this universe will be reduced to the size of a pin.\n\nAlthough we appear to be somewhat isolated from the time effects, the Sphere is obviously permeable. By synchronizing our computer with the [Fleet.GetName] outside the rift, we should be able to preserve the rapidly disappearing data. As a more drastic measure, we could use the unique properties of astral thread to repolarize the hull and seal the temporal leak.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:97`

## astral_rift.1210.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1210.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

皱缩的行星和丢失的数据还只是问题的开始。这些麻烦逐渐影响到了船员的记忆，许多人开始感到困惑，忘记自己在做什么研究或者甚至忘记了自己进入过裂隙。\n\n$astral_rift.1210.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

Shrinking plants and lost data were only the start of our issues. The trouble has spread to the memory of the crew, with many appearing confused, forgetting what they were working on, or even that they entered the rift at all.\n\n$astral_rift.1210.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:98`

## astral_rift.1210.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1210.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

皱缩的行星和丢失的数据还只是问题的开始。裂隙另一端的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]还遭遇了一些异常的事件。集体经验变得困惑，我们发现非常难以进行同步。\n\n$astral_rift.1210.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

Shrinking plants and lost data were only the start of our issues. The [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural] on the far side of the rift are experiencing something unusual. Our collective experience is growing confused, and we are finding it increasingly difficult to synchronize.\n\n$astral_rift.1210.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:99`

## astral_rift.1210.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1210.a&lang=en)

与[Fleet.GetName]进行同步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

Synchronize with the [Fleet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:100`

## astral_rift.1210.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1210.b&lang=en)

### 中文

重极化船体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

Repolarize the hull.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:101`

## astral_rift.1215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

紧急情况

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

Emergency

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:103`

## astral_rift.1215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[fleet.ship.GetName]迅速作出了反应；我们现在定期发送最新消息，并成功地保存了我们迄今为止收集到的数据。\n\n不幸的是，我们仍然暴露在异常时间流中，而且影响越来越强……\n\n随着我们的宇航员衰老的迹象越来越明显，我们要从这个宇宙中战略性地撤退，以免为时过晚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

The [fleet.ship.GetName] was prompt to respond; we are now sending updates at regular intervals. This has successfully preserved the data we've collected thus far.\n\nUnfortunately, we're still exposed to the anomalous temporal flow, and the effects are getting stronger...\n\nWith significant and accelerating signs of de-aging in our crew, we are tactically retreating from this universe before it is too late.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:104`

## astral_rift.1220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

反向诞生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

Unbirthing

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:106`

## astral_rift.1220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的船体成功地进行了重极化。我们现在可以与最极端的时间膨胀效应隔绝开了。我们的思维模式回归到了正常，任务得以继续进行。\n\n我们现在准备对这个宇宙的独特物理进行充分研究。\n\n我们可以驶近其中一颗恒星，直接观测它的“反诞生”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

The §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!'s hull has been successfully repolarized. We are now well isolated from the most extreme effects of the time dilation. Our thought patterns have returned to normal and we are able to continue the mission.\n\nWe are now prepared to study the unique physics of this universe more fully.\n\nWe could navigate closer toward one of the stars for direct observation of its 'unbirthing.' On the other hand, the very edge of existence would be a perfect vantage point to watch the end of time.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:107`

## astral_rift.1220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

靠近最近一个恒星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

Approach the closest star system.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:108`

## astral_rift.1220.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1220.b&lang=en)

### 中文

从边缘观测这个宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

Observe from the edge of the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:109`

## astral_rift.1225.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1225.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星尘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

Stardust

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:111`

## astral_rift.1225.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1225.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们靠近了一颗逐渐变得暗淡的G型主序星。随着光线减弱，它本该释放的气体一直在向回收缩。要在我们自己的宇宙中观察到这一点需要数百万年的时间。这是在合理的时间跨度内见证这一事件的独特机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

We have approached a Class G star, which is slowly fading away. The gases it normally emits have been retracting as its light weakens. It would take millions of years to observe this in our own universe. This is a unique opportunity to witness such an event within a reasonable span of time.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:112`

## astral_rift.1225.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1225.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1225.desc.common$\n\n船员们目睹这一奇妙现象时的兴奋溢于言表。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

$astral_rift.1225.desc.common$\n\nThe crew's excitement at witnessing this wonderful phenomenon is palpable.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:113`

### English

$astral_rift.1225.desc.common$

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1225.a&lang=en)

过了这个村就没这个店了。

### English

There may never be another chance.

## astral_rift.1230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

始之终末

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

End of the Beginning

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:117`

## astral_rift.1230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经超越了宇宙的视界，这是通常不可能实现的壮举。\n\n在宇宙的边缘，是一种超越一切理性的绝对的虚无。\n\n向中心望去，我们看到一切都汇聚到空间的一个点上。结果我们收集到的物理数据简直是天文数字。\n\n然而，再拖延下去可能是致命的错误，我们要马上回来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:92`

### English

We have traveled far beyond the cosmic horizon, a feat that would normally be impossible for us to achieve.\n\nAt the edge of the universe was an absolute nothingness that extended past all rationality.\n\nLooking towards the center, we witnessed everything coalesce toward a single point in space. The physics data we have collected as a result is literally astronomical.\n\nHowever, lingering any further might prove a fatal mistake, and we are returning immediately.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:118`

## astral_rift.1230.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1230.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是美妙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

Wondrous.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:119`

## astral_rift.1235.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1235.name&lang=en)

### 中文

始之终末

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

End of the Beginning

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:121`

## astral_rift.1235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

再也没有什么星星了。\n\n我们成功地记录了诞生的每一个细节，由终至始。构成燃烧核心物质现在是一团原子迷雾的涡流。从中获取的物理知识不可估量。\n\n然而，再拖延下去可能是致命的错误，我们要马上回来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

The star is no more.\n\nWe have managed to record almost every moment of its creation, from end to beginning. What once comprised a burning core is now a swirling mist of atoms. The physics knowledge we have gained is invaluable.\n\nHowever, lingering any further might prove a fatal mistake, and we are returning immediately.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:122`

## astral_rift.1235.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1235.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是美妙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Wondrous.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:123`

流体

### English

## astral_rift.1300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

流体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

The Fluid

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:130`

## astral_rift.1300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!被淹没在一种粘稠的红色液体中。在我们周围漂浮着巨大的圆盘状固体物质，但到目前为止，我们还没有与它们相撞。一层半凝胶状物质的固体墙四面包围着我们。我们轻松地滑行。\n\n我们从裂隙中感受到的有节奏的脉冲与我们周围液体的谐波频率是同步的。每隔三秒钟，我们就会被一股压力向前推进，迫使我们更深地进入管状系统。我们的引擎很难抵抗液流。\n\n这是一个收集样本的好机会，但这些的设备可能会阻碍我们在扭曲的隧道中航行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

Our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! has arrived submerged inside of a viscous, reddish fluid. All around us drift large disc-shaped blobs of more solid matter, but we have avoided collision with them thus far. A solid wall of a semi-gelatinous material surrounds us on all sides. We glide along easily.\n\nThe rhythmic pulsing we sensed from within this rift is in sync with the harmonic frequency of the liquid around us. Every three seconds, we are pushed forward with a rush of pressure driving us deeper into the tubular system. Resisting the flow is quite difficult on our engines.\n\nThis is a great opportunity for sample collection, but the appropriate apparatus may impede our navigation in these twisting tunnels.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:131`

## astral_rift.1300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

部署样品收集拖网。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Deploy sample collection rig.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:132`

## astral_rift.1300.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1300.b&lang=en)

### 中文

放心地继续前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

Proceed forward at ease.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:133`

## astral_rift.1305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

受阻

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Snagged

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:135`

## astral_rift.1305.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1305.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在进入裂隙后一段时间，我们突然停了下来，拖在我们身后的取样设备卡在了隧道系统的狭窄处。\n\n我们试图用断断续续的推进力来挣脱，但是水流的力量使我们不能取得任何有意义的进展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

Some time after arriving within the rift, we came to an abrupt halt as the sample apparatus trawling behind us became lodged in a narrowing of the tunnel system.\n\nWe attempted to free ourselves with intermittent bursts of propulsion, but the strength of the current prevented us from making any meaningful progress.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:136`

## astral_rift.1305.desc.no_gases

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1305.desc.no_gases&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1305.desc.common$\n\n我们别无选择只能切断取样设备以继续前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

$astral_rift.1305.desc.common$\n\nWe have no choice but to simply cut the sample apparatus free and continue onward.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:137`

## astral_rift.1305.desc.gases

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1305.desc.gases&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1305.desc.common$\n\n最安全的选择是切断取样设备，继续前进。然而，如果给我们的推进器注入一些异星天然气，我们还是有可能保存样本的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

$astral_rift.1305.desc.common$\n\nThe safest option may be to simply cut the sample apparatus free and continue onward. However, it may still be possible to preserve the sample by supplementing our propulsion with a charge of exotic gases.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:138`

## astral_rift.1305.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1305.a&lang=en)

### 中文

把它切断。不值得冒这么大风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

Cut it loose. The risk isn't worth it.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:139`

## astral_rift.1305.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1305.b&lang=en)

使用紧急燃气。

### English

Use emergency gases.

## astral_rift.1310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舱室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

The Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:142`

## astral_rift.1310.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1310.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

沿着隧道壁的层流使我们在航行过程中良好地保持在中心。几天过去了，没有发生什么大事。我们周围的盘状异物也被困在液流终；它们轻轻飘过球体，翻滚而过。\n\n我们没有看到任何有知觉生命存在的证据。事实上，在这一点上，我们最强烈的假设是我们在一些未知的，巨大的超级有机体中。如果我们的存在被任何自然防御系统注意到，我们也还没有看到。\n\n我们现在发现自己置身于一个巨大的房间。壁上排列着静脉状突起。腔壁有规律地膨胀和收缩，迫使数百万升的液体通过一个出口。\n\n当我们尝试维持位置时，发现了第二个进入舱室的入口。微量的结晶物质悬浮在从隧道流出的液体中。水流的压力很低，只要用一些巧妙的导航，就可以探测到这个孔口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

The laminar flow along the tunnel walls has kept us well centered in our journey. Several days have passed without much incident. The disc-shaped foreign bodies surrounding us are also caught within the flow; they gently drift and tumble past the Sphere.\n\nWe have witnessed no evidence of sentient life. In fact, our strongest supposition at this point is that we are inside of some unknown, massive super organism. If our presence has been noticed by any natural defenses, we have not yet witnessed it.\n\nWe now find ourselves in an enormous chamber. The walls are lined with veiny protrusions. The chamber walls regularly expand and contract, forcing millions of liters of fluid through a single exit.\n\nAs we held our position, we discovered a second entryway into the chamber. Trace amounts of crystalline matter are suspended in the fluid coming from this tunnel. The pressure of its flow is low enough that with some skillful navigating this orifice could be explored.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:143`

## astral_rift.1310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们切断了取样网，然后注意到装置周围的区域似乎慢慢在变黑，并且逐渐被吞没。\n\n$astral_rift.1310.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

We have cut loose the sampling rig. We noticed the surrounding area around the apparatus seemed to slowly darken and became swollen over time.\n\n$astral_rift.1310.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:144`

## astral_rift.1310.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1310.a&lang=en)

### 中文

随着液流出去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

Follow the flow outward.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:145`

### 中文

探索结晶孔。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Explore the crystalline orifice.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:146`

$astral_rift.1310.name$

### English

$astral_rift.1310.name$

## astral_rift.1311.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1311.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

向推进器注入异星天然气，我们终于将取样装置从管道壁上扯下来，由此我们可以取回这些宝贵的样品。\n\n$astral_rift.1310.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

By supplementing our propulsion with a charge of exotic gases, we were able to rip the apparatus free from the tunnel wall. We have since been able to recover the valuable samples.\n\n$astral_rift.1310.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:149`

### English

## astral_rift.1315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

团块

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

The Blob

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:153`

## astral_rift.1315.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1315.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

突然，几百米外出现了一个星舰大小的无定形团块。我们试图从远处观察它却引起了它的注意。\n\n实体通过膜把§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!迅速吸收。它的内部主要由一种腐蚀性酶组成；我们说话这会儿，球体的船体正在被消化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

Suddenly, an amorphous blob the size of a starship was observed several hundred meters away. Attempting to observe it from a distance, we somehow managed to draw its attention.\n\nThe §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! was quickly absorbed through the entity's membrane. Its interior is composed mostly of a corrosive enzyme; the Sphere's hull is being digested as we speak.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:154`

## astral_rift.1315.desc_no_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1315.desc_no_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的战术指挥官建议立即撤离，但我们的生物学家认为可以通过结合我们手头的气体和液体来中和其中的酶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

Our tactical officer recommends immediate evacuation, but our biologist theorizes its enzymes can be neutralized by combining the gases and fluid we have at our disposal.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:155`

## astral_rift.1315.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1315.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

也许需要紧急撤离，但我们也推测可以通过适当的气体和液体组合来中和其中的酶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

Evacuation may be appropriate, but we theorize its enzymes can be neutralized with the right combination of gases and fluid we have at our disposal.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:156`

## astral_rift.1315.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1315.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

在洞口的另一边，我们进入了另一个腔体。这个舱室的规模使我们迄今为止见过的任何腔体都相形见绌。这里，细而精致的分枝卷须向各个方向伸展。它们都是如此错综复杂地交织在一起，我们很难再前进一步。这种环境中的流体充满了气体，这对我们来说很容易收集。\n\n$astral_rift.1315.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

On the far side of the orifice, we entered another cavity. This chamber dwarfed any other we had seen thus far. Here, thin and delicately branching tendrils stretch out in every direction. They are all so intricately woven together that it has become difficult to proceed. The fluid in this environment is saturated with gases which have been easy for us to collect.\n\n$astral_rift.1315.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:158`

## astral_rift.1316.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1316.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

一路与流体抗争，我们终于挤进了上一个房间的一个分支。我们进入了一个由颤抖的水晶组成的洞穴，周围的墙壁过滤了来自各个方向的液体，留下了结晶残留物。\n\n$astral_rift.1315.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

Fighting through the current, we were able to squeeze into an off-shoot from the previous chamber. We emerged into a cavern of quivering crystals. The walls around us filtered fluid from every direction, causing a crystalline residue to be left behind.\n\n$astral_rift.1315.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:165`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

提取

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

Extraction

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:171`

## astral_rift.1320.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1320.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管这个生物酸性的酶对船体很危险，但我们有很长的时间来启动紧急锚，逃回到裂隙的另一端去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Though the creature's acidic enzymes were a danger to the hull, we had plenty of time to engage the emergency anchor and escape back through the other side of the rift.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:172`

## astral_rift.1320.desc.no_rig

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1320.desc.no_rig&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1320.desc.common$\n\n不过，我们的航行也并非全是徒劳。在我们的船体上发现了大量来自那头野兽的物质。我们的生物学家希望能调查其中包含的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

$astral_rift.1320.desc.common$\n\nOur voyage was not all in vain, however. A significant amount of material from the beast was found still attached to our hull. Our biologists are eager to investigate what secrets it may contain.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:173`

## astral_rift.1320.desc.rig

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1320.desc.rig&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1320.desc.common$\n\n不过，我们的航行也并非全是徒劳。我们的取样网中回收了大量来自那头野兽的物质。我们的生物学家希望能调查其中包含的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

$astral_rift.1320.desc.common$\n\nOur voyage was not all in vain, however. A significant amount of material from the beast was recovered by our sampling rig. Our biologists are eager to investigate what secrets it may contain.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:174`

## astral_rift.1320.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1320.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

把酸性中和掉，然后给我们来一杯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

Neutralize the acid. Then we shall drink.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:175`

## astral_rift.1325.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1325.name&lang=en)

### 中文

溶解

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

Dissolution

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:177`

## astral_rift.1325.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1325.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管其尺寸巨大，但这头生物的生物学却极度原始。\n\n在释放出我们独特的混合气体后不久，它的膜就溶解了。它的细胞完整性被破坏，内容物逸散到周围的液流中，直到只剩下巨大的核心。\n\n我们很高兴地报告对这个物体的成功回收，它与我们维度中许多单细胞生物的细胞核并没有完全不同。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

While significant in size, the creature turned out to be remarkably primitive in its biology.\n\nVery soon after releasing our unique concoction of gases, its membrane dissolved. No longer able to maintain cellular integrity, its internals were carried away into the surrounding currents until only the massive core remained.\n\nWe are pleased to report that we have successfully recovered the object, which is not wholly unlike the nucleus of many single-celled organisms from our own dimension.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:178`

## astral_rift.1325.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1325.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们猜测这个生物是这个巨大的管道和液体网络中的某种抗体，它将我们视为外来威胁。我们带着战利品伤痕累累地回来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

Our speculation is that this creature was some sort of antibody in this colossal network of tubes and fluid, and identified us as a foreign threat. Damaged by the encounter, we have returned with our trophy.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:179`

## astral_rift.1325.desc.no_rig

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1325.desc.no_rig&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.1$\n\n$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.1$\n\n$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:180`

## astral_rift.1325.desc.rig

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1325.desc.rig&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.1$\n\n此外，此前救回来的取样网中可以取回大量来自那头野兽的物质。我们的生物学家迫不及待地想研究其中隐藏的秘密了。\n\n$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.1$\n\nIn addition, the sampling rig we managed to save previously has been able to recover a significant amount of material from the beast. Our biologists are eager to investigate what secrets it may contain.\n\n$astral_rift.1325.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:181`

## astral_rift.1330.name

### 中文

溶解

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

Dissolved

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:183`

## astral_rift.1330.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1330.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与[explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural]的联络中断了。\n\n按照标准流程，我们拉回了锚链，取回了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的残余部分。\n\n它几乎被完全溶解，大量高浓度生物质包裹着每一寸残骸。我们确信这可以带来新的科技突破。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

We have lost contact with our [explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural].\n\nAs is standard procedure, we have retracted the anchor and recovered what was left of the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nWhile it is mostly dissolved, there is a highly concentrated biomass coating all remaining pieces. We are certain this will lead to new breakthroughs.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:184`

## astral_rift.1330.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1330.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与科研团队的联络中断了。\n\n按照标准流程，我们拉回了锚链，取回了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的残余部分。\n\n它几乎被完全溶解，大量高浓度生物质包裹着每一寸残骸。我们的生物学家确信这可以带来新的科技突破。\n\n我们赞扬全体船员的勇敢。他们的名字将被铭记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

We have lost contact with the Science team.\n\nAs is standard procedure, we have retracted the anchor and recovered what was left of the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nWhile it is mostly dissolved, there is a highly concentrated biomass coating all remaining pieces. Our biologists are certain this will lead to new breakthroughs.\n\nWe commend the crew for its bravery. Their names will be remembered.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:185`

## infinity_root_leader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfinity_root_leader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!从$r_infinity_root$的种子中诞生。[Root.GetSheHeCap]刚刚来到这个世界，但已经拥有了可观的智力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! was born from the seeds of the $r_infinity_root$. [Root.GetSheHeCap] is new to this world, but possesses a remarkable intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:190`

## garden_astral_rift_resurrected_leader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agarden_astral_rift_resurrected_leader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!不知为何从一场失败的星界裂隙旅途中回来，这个星界裂隙充满各种植被。尽管[Root.GetHeShe]已经同过去的自己样貌大不一样，但[Root.GetHeShe]仍保留着相似的特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! somehow returned to us from a failed expedition into an Astral Rift abundant with vegetation. While [Root.GetHeShe] no longer resemble[Root.GetSXX] [Root.GetHisHer] former self, [Root.GetHeShe] seem[Root.GetSXX] to possess similar qualities.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:191`

## astral_rift_garden

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift_garden&lang=en)

花园

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

The Garden

## astral_rift.1400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

荆棘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

Thorns

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:195`

## astral_rift.1400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们来到了一间长满绿色藤蔓的房间。\n\n带刺的荆棘几乎从每一个弯曲处张牙舞爪地伸出。\n\n我们很快意识到这些生物有某种意图，或者至少是一种防御本能。\n\n我们注意到大气中的氧含量异常高。我们推测，烧掉这些讨厌的东西将应该很容易。\n\n另一方面，这也肯定会破坏我们本可以收集到的样本。我们观察到大量的毛茸茸的果实挂在茎上，这可以为我们提供对这种植物群的大量见解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

We arrived in a chamber of writhing green vines.\n\nBarbed thorns bite outward from nearly every bend and twist in their coils.\n\nWe quickly became aware that these organisms have some sort of intent about them, or at least a defensive instinct.\n\nWe have noted an abnormally high oxygen content in the atmosphere. We speculate that burning away these nuisances would prove to be an easy task.\n\nOn the other hand, this would certainly destroy any samples we would otherwise be able to collect. We've observed a large quantity of hairy fruit hanging from the stalks that could provide a generous amount of insight into this flora.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:196`

## astral_rift.1400.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1400.a&lang=en)

### 中文

烧了这些杂草。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

Burn the weeds.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:197`

## astral_rift.1400.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1400.b&lang=en)

### 中文

提取果实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Extract the fruit.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:198`

## astral_rift.1405.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1405.name&lang=en)

### 中文

身陷囹圄

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

Entangled

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:200`

## astral_rift.1405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们被困住了。\n\n§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的光滑表面让这些植物无处下藤，但我们的锚链却被各个方向神来的纤维藤蔓层层包裹。\n\n尽管有这些障碍，如果我们想安全返回并离开裂隙，任何植物物质都无法阻挡我们的退路。我们随身携带的生物物质可能会有些用处。\n\n然而，如果我们想继续向前探索，我们唯一实际的选择将是烧毁树叶。由此产生的火焰将非常强烈。这么近的距离，我们的船有受损的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

We have become entangled.\n\nWhile the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!'s smooth surface provides little purchase for the plants, our anchor cable has become wrapped from all directions in fibrous vines.\n\nDespite these obstructions, no plant matter could overcome our retraction if we want to return to safety and leave the rift. The biological matter we pull with us could likely be of some use.\n\nHowever, if we want to continue exploring forward, our only practical option will be to burn through the foliage. The resulting blaze will be highly intense. At such close proximity, we risk damaging our vessel.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:201`

## astral_rift.1405.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1405.a&lang=en)

### 中文

烧掉这些缠绕的藤蔓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Burn the entangling vines.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:202`

## astral_rift.1405.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1405.b&lang=en)

### 中文

起锚返航。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

Pull anchor. Escape.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:203`

## astral_rift.1410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

空谷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

The Hollow

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:205`

## astral_rift.1410.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1410.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在处理了眼前的危险之后，我们更彻底地探索了我们周围的环境。\n\n我们在一系列洞穴的深处。\n\n我们通过附近的一条隧道，进入了一个更广阔的山谷。像根一样的植物丛悬挂在天花板上，将粘性液体滴到下面的叶子上，然后被更多的植物吸收。它有自己繁荣的生态系统。形成这样一个既定的环境肯定需要几千年的时间。\n\n这个地区的卷须比我们刚到的地方还要密集。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Having dealt with the immediate danger, we explored our surroundings more thoroughly.\n\nWe are deep within a series of caverns.\n\nScouting our way through a nearby tunnel led us into a more expansive hollow. Systems of root-like clusters dangle from the ceiling, dripping a viscous fluid onto the foliage below - which then becomes absorbed by even more vegetation. It is its own thriving ecosystem. To develop such an established environment must have taken several millennia.\n\nThe tendrils in this area are even more densely packed than where we arrived.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:206`

## astral_rift.1410.desc.burn

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1410.desc.burn&lang=en)

### 中文

不出所料，空气中高浓度的氧气产生了一阵火焰，迅速吞噬了带刺的植物。\n\n摆脱了阻碍，我们继续探索。我们能够从裂隙边缘收集到大量与星界物质混合在一起的灰烬。\n\n$astral_rift.1410.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

As expected, the high oxygen concentrations in the air produced a burst of flame that quickly consumed the prickly plants.\n\nUnencumbered, we continued exploring. We were able to recover much of the ash that had become mixed with astral material from the rift's edge.\n\n$astral_rift.1410.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:207`

## astral_rift.1410.desc.extract

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1410.desc.extract&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一边穿过藤蔓，一边用我们的样本收集工具从茎上剪下一些果实。每次我们拔掉一根，剩下的部分就会无力地倒在一边。我们最后收集到了足够多的果实，也建立了一个大范围的安全区域。\n\n这些植物的“果实”实际上更多的是一种真菌，似乎共生于整个根系中。我们带了一些样品到船上作进一步研究。\n\n我们推测这种真菌是它们所占据的植物的一种神经簇。\n\n$astral_rift.1410.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

As we weaved our way through the vines, we were able to shear some of the fruit from their stems using our sample collection tools. Each time we removed one, the rest of the plant fell limply to the side. Eventually we gathered enough to establish a wide area of safety.\n\nThe "fruit" of these plants are actually more of a symbiotic fungal growth that seems present throughout the entire root system. We have taken some samples aboard for further study.\n\nWe speculate this fungus serves as a kind of neural cluster for the plants they occupy.\n\n$astral_rift.1410.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:208`

## astral_rift.1410.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1410.a&lang=en)

全部烧掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

Burn it all.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:209`

研究这个地方。

Study this place.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1415.name&lang=en)

### 中文

熊熊烈焰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

Conflagration

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:212`

## astral_rift.1415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很不幸，我们与§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!上的科学团队失去了联系。\n\n在回收时，球体的大半已经完全熔为残渣。所有的船员都不幸牺牲了。\n\n我们重新在科研船上安装了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!以对星界位面进行其他的探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

Unfortunately, we lost contact with the science team aboard the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nUpon recovery, half of the vessel had completely melted into slag. All aboard have perished.\n\nWe have refitted a new §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! to the science ship for further explorations into the astral planes.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:213`

## astral_rift.1420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1420.name&lang=en)

逃离

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:215`

## astral_rift.1420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个地方固有的危险不值得去冒险。\n\n我们已拉回锚链，让我们的[explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural]回到了安全的地方。\n\n我们找到的样本有一些独特有趣的特性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

The inherent danger of this place was not worth the risk.\n\nWe have pulled the retrievable anchor and returned our [explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural] to safety.\n\nThe samples we have recovered have some uniquely interesting properties.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:216`

## astral_rift.1420.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1420.b.evopred&lang=en)

### English

### English

## astral_rift.1425.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1425.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们用烈火清理了这个洞穴的大部分区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

We have cleared out most of this chamber with flame.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:220`

## astral_rift.1425.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1425.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

这篇栖息地没有雷暴之类的东西，这些生物可能已经完全适应了一个没有燃烧威胁的环境。\n\n最后的火焰熄灭后不久，一个奇怪的生物从灰烬中出现。它有几百条肢体，在余烬中慌乱地爬行。爪子般的附属物在灰烬中辛勤地筛着。一种柔和的、低沉的声音从它身体上的几片膜里发出来。\n\n然后它把注意力转向了我们。头向一侧倾斜，一排鳞状的刺从颈部向外伸出。这只闪闪发光的动物，向我们发出急促而有节奏的声音。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

With no exposure to thunderstorms in this habitat, these organisms have likely completely adapted to an environment where there is never a threat of ignition.\n\nShortly after the last of the flames died away, a strange creature emerged from the ashes. With its hundreds of limbs, it crawled among the embers in confusion. Claw-like appendages sifted through the ashes diligently. A soft, low sound shot forth from several of the membranes coating the segments of its body.\n\nIt then turned its attention toward us. Head tilted to the side, an arrangement of scaled spines lifted outward from its neck. With quick, rhythmic pulses, the shimmering beast sent audible sounds in our direction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:221`

## astral_rift.1425.desc.previousburn

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1425.desc.previousburn&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.1$\n\n和刚才一样，这些植物在一场巨大的大火中迅速瓦解。我们还观察到，覆盖在这些植物表面的液体起到了助燃剂的作用。\n\n$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.1$\n\nAs before, the plants disintegrated in a massive conflagration. We have additionally observed that the fluid coating these plants serves as an accelerant.\n\n$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:222`

## astral_rift.1425.desc.nopreviousburn

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1425.desc.nopreviousburn&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.1$\n\n如同预期，空气中高浓度的氧气燃气了熊熊烈火，瞬间就吞没了这些多刺植物。\n\n$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.1$\n\nAs expected, the high oxygen concentration in the air produced a burst of flame that quickly consumed the prickly plants.\n\n$astral_rift.1425.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:223`

未知生物

### English

A Creature

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:228`

## astral_rift.1430.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1430.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们尽了最大的努力去研究密室，但还是被一头潜在的危险生物发现了。\n\n我们起先发现它在荆棘中，用它那数百条肢体精确地爬行。随着它在每株植物上寻找果实并进行栽培，一种柔和而尖锐的声音从它身体上的几层膜里发出来。\n\n不幸的是，我们的船壳撞在一丛又脆又宽的叶子上。听到声音后，它迅速把注意力转向了我们。头向一侧倾斜，一排鳞状的刺从颈部向外伸出。这只闪闪发光的动物，向我们这边发出急促而有节奏的声音。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Despite our most careful efforts to study the chamber, we have been discovered by a potentially dangerous creature.\n\nWe first spotted it crawling among the thorns with delicate precision using its hundreds of limbs. A soft, shrill sound shot forth from several of the membranes coating the segments of its body as it tended to the fruit it found in every plant, cultivating them.\n\nUnfortunately, our hull brushed harshly against a clump of crisp, broad leaves. Alerted by the noise, it quickly turned its attention toward us. Head tilted to the side, an arrangement of scaled spines lifted outward from its neck. With quick, rhythmic pulses, the shimmering beast sent audible sounds in our direction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:229`

### English

### English

## astral_rift.1435.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1435.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，我们已经引起了这只生物的注意。它的头向一侧倾斜，一排鳞状的刺从颈部向外伸出。这只闪闪发光的动物，向我们这边发出急促而有节奏的声音。\n\n我们没办法再继续研究它了。\n\n我们预测它面对在强烈的火焰是，应该也会如同这里大多数的生物一样脆弱。不过，现在最好还是逃走，减少损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

Unfortunately, we have drawn the attention of the creature. Head tilted to the side, an arrangement of scaled spines lifted outward from its neck. With quick, rhythmic pulses, the shimmering beast sent audible sounds in our direction.\n\nWe'll be unable to study it any further.\n\nWe predict it would be quite vulnerable if exposed to intense flame, as most of the other life around here tends to be. However, it may be best to escape now and cut our losses.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:234`

A Creature

## astral_rift.1440.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1440.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有什么东西潜伏在密林之中。\n\n当我们找到一个隐蔽的位置来研究这个空间时，一只奇怪的生物很快从树叶中出现。我们起先发现它在荆棘中，用它那数百条肢体精确地爬行。随着它在每株植物上寻找果实并进行栽培，一种柔和而尖锐的声音从它身体上的几层膜里发出来。\n\n我们已经观察了一段时间，并相信进一步的观察肯定能带来宝贵的研究机会。\n\n我们预测它面对在强烈的火焰是，应该也会如同这里大多数的生物一样脆弱。如果我们有机会，或许最好还是结果它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

There is something lurking among the leaves.\n\nAs we assumed a surreptitious position to study this chamber, a strange creature soon emerged from the foliage. We first spotted it crawling among the thorns with delicate precision using its hundreds of limbs. A soft, shrill sound shot forth from several of the membranes coating the segments of its body as it tended to the fruit it found in every plant, cultivating them.\n\nWe have been observing it for some time now, and believe further study would warrant valuable research opportunities.\n\nOn the other hand, we predict it would be quite vulnerable if exposed to intense flame, as most of the other life around here tends to be. It may be best to exterminate it while we have the opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:239`

行星物质

### English

Plant Material

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:243`

## astral_rift.1445.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1445.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们失去了与§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的联系。\n\n按照标准流程，我们拉回了锚链并取回了舰船。\n\n里面没有发现任何我们[explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural]残余物的痕迹。然而，他们的特征成分可以在现在占据整个机舱内部的巨大根系中检测到。\n\n球体本身已被运送到其他地方进行进一步研究。也许我们可以把这些植物种在什么地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

We have lost contact with the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nAs is standard procedure, we have retracted the anchor and recovered the vessel.\n\nThere is no evidence of any of our [explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural] remaining. However, their signature components can be detected among the massive root system that now occupies the entire inside of the cabin.\n\nThe sphere itself has been transported elsewhere for further study. Perhaps we could plant it somewhere.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:244`

## astral_rift.1445.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1445.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的牺牲不会白费。可以解剖了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Their sacrifice was not in vain. Now for the dissection.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:245`

## astral_rift.1450.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1450.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无限之根

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

Infinity Root

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:247`

## astral_rift.1450.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1450.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这家伙对火并不陌生。\n\n我们一点燃第一缕火苗，它就惊慌地跑开了——动作之快，一定是出于某种本能。\n\n也许它早就意识到了这个环境的易燃特性。不管是什么原因，它已经逃到了不知道哪个犄角旮旯里以至于我们在任何传感器范围内都找不到它的任何迹象。\n\n它离开以后，我们找到了它撤退时掉下的一件东西。应该是因为太重了带不动所以被丢了下来。\n\n我们简单的实验表明，无论把这个物体放在哪里，它都会立即长出微小的卷须，寻找新的地方定居和繁衍。只要有水分，这些卷须就会聚集起来茁壮成长。\n\n令人震惊的是，由此产生的植物显示出独特的智慧迹象，即使是在嫩芽时期。\n\n我们觉得它甚至可以成为我们帝国的宝贵资产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

The creature was no stranger to fire.\n\nAs soon as we sparked the first flame, it skittered away in alarm - a behavior so quick it must be some sort of instinct.\n\nPerhaps it was aware of the flammable properties of this environment. Whatever the reason, it has retreated so far into the cracks and crevices of this place that we no longer detect any sign of it within any range of sensors.\n\nAfter it departed, we recovered an object it dropped during its retreat. It must have been too much of a burden to carry along.\n\nOur brief experiments have shown that wherever this object is placed, it immediately begins sending out microscopic tendrils to scout out new areas to settle and flourish. At the merest detection of moisture, those tendrils congregate and thrive.\n\nShockingly, the resulting plant exhibits unique signs of intelligence, even as a young sprout.\n\nWe suspect it could even be raised as a valuable asset to our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:248`

## astral_rift.1450.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1450.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

完美。无论怎么取样它都会再生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

Perfect. No matter how much we sample, it regenerates.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:249`

## astral_rift.1455.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1455.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们偷偷地观察了这个生物在这座隐蔽花园里的辛勤劳作许久，不知道我们还能在这多久不被发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

We have been stealthily observing the creature as it goes about its duties in this secluded garden. It is uncertain how long we can remain here undetected.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:252`

## astral_rift.1455.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1455.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1455.desc.common$\n\n这个生物的每个体节都长着一对触角。这些触角可以帮他在所种植植物的纤毛上探测并移除害虫。这些触角的解剖结构超出了我们对生物器官功能的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

$astral_rift.1455.desc.common$\n\nEach segment of the creature contains a pair of antennae. These assist it in detecting and removing pests from the minute hairs of the fruit it cultivates. The anatomical structure of these has furthered our understanding of what biological organisms are capable of.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:253`

## astral_rift.1455.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1455.a&lang=en)
