继续研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Continued Studies.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:254`

## astral_rift.1456.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1456.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深入研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Continued Studies

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:256`

## astral_rift.1456.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1456.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1455.desc.common$\n\n我们注意到它用一对巨大的前肢把一个类似于“果实”的有机团块紧紧地抓在胸前。有几次，我们观察到它用下颌骨挖进这个物体，并把它的一小部分移植到附近的植物上。它显然正在人工繁殖这些植物。这解释了这些植物为什么缺乏传粉媒介或可识别的繁殖方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

$astral_rift.1455.desc.common$\n\nWe've noticed an organic mass resembling the "fruit" clutched tightly to the creature's chest, held in its massive pair of forelimbs. Several times, we have observed its mandibles dig into this object and graft small parts of it onto the nearby vegetation. It is propagating these plants artificially. This explains the lack of pollinators or identifiable method of reproduction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:257`

## astral_rift.1457.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1457.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无限之根

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Infinity Root

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:259`

## astral_rift.1457.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1457.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1455.desc.common$\n\n这个生物挖了一个小洞，把它拿着的东西放了进去。飞奔而去的同时，它用数百条腿把这个东西埋在了一块浅土里。\n\n在确定这个生物不会很快回来后，我们把它拿走了。\n\n我们简单的实验表明，无论放在哪里，这种材料都会立即发出微小的卷须，寻找新的地方扎根和生长。只要有水分，这些卷须就会聚集起来茁壮成长。\n\n令人震惊的是，由此产生的植物显示出独特的智慧迹象，即使是在嫩芽时期。\n\n我们觉得它甚至可以成为我们帝国的宝贵资产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

$astral_rift.1455.desc.common$\n\nThe creature has dug a small burrow, placing the object it was holding inside. As it skittered away, its hundreds of legs buried it in a shallow lump of soil.\n\nAfter making sure the creature would not be returning soon, we were able to recover it entirely.\n\nOur brief experiments have shown that wherever it is placed, the material immediately begins sending out microscopic tendrils to scout out new areas to settle and flourish. At the merest detection of moisture, those tendrils congregate and thrive.\n\nShockingly, the resulting plant exhibits unique signs of intelligence, even as a young sprout.\n\nWe suspect it could even be raised as a valuable asset to our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:260`

## astral_rift.1465.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1465.name&lang=en)

### 中文

种下种子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Seeds Sown

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:262`

## astral_rift.1465.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1465.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几年前，§Y[from.GetName]§!在一次对某个充满植被的星界裂隙进行探险的过程中，被异化变成了一团树根和叶子。今天，此人的同事宣布了一个了不起的突破，此人已经恢复了§Y[from.GetName]§!的心智！\n\n尽管[From.GetHeShe]已经不再记得[From.GetHisHer]从前的自我，但[From.GetHeShe]仍保留着相似的特质。[From.GetHimHer]的眼前展开了全新的人生画卷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Several years ago, §Y[from.GetName]§! was botanomorphed into a mass of roots and leaves during a failed expedition into an Astral Rift abundant with vegetation. Today their colleagues announced a marvelous breakthrough, they have restored §Y[from.GetName]'s§! mind!\n\nWhile [From.GetHeShe] no longer resemble[From.GetSXX] [From.GetHisHer] former self, [From.GetHeShe] seem[From.GetSXX] to possess similar qualities. A whole new life lies ahead of [From.GetHimHer].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:263`

## astral_rift.1465.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1465.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!回来为我们效力了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]§! has returned to our service!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:264`

### English

## astral_rift.1500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怪异控制台

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Strange Console

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:271`

## astral_rift.1500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃的建筑物杂乱无章地散落在沙漠上。\n\n从其中最大的一个巨大的圆顶里面，发出微弱的黄光。从地板到天花板，室内弯曲的墙壁上覆盖着一种未知的文字。\n\n在主房间的中央是一个控制台，上面有两个大按钮：一个深蓝，一个亮绿。我们正在破译墙上的文字，看看它是否能给我们关于这个控制台可能是做什么的提示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Abandoned buildings are scattered haphazardly across the sands.\n\nFrom inside the largest of these, a massive dome, came a faint yellow light. From floor to ceiling, the interior's curving walls were covered in an unknown script.\n\nAt the center of the main chamber is a console with two large buttons: one deep blue, the other a vibrant green. We are in the process of deciphering the text on the walls, to see if it gives us an indication of what this console might be for.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:272`

## astral_rift.1505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被困时光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

Trapped in Time

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:274`

## astral_rift.1505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从上一次听到[Leader.GetName]的消息已经过去了很长时间。继续等待他们回来已经没什么意义了。\n\n我们收回锚链，把空空的球体拉回到我们的次元。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

Too much time has passed since we last heard from [Leader.GetName]. There is no point in continuing to wait for their return.\n\nWe retracted the anchor cable and pulled the empty Sphere back into our dimension.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:275`

## astral_rift.1505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1505.a&lang=en)

有多大概率呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

What were the odds?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:276`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1510.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

……穹顶内部的地面泛着微弱的黄光。一种位置的文字从上而下覆盖满墙。\n\n在主房间的中心是一个控制台，上面有两个大按钮，一个深蓝，一个亮绿。我们已经开始我们正在破译墙上的文字，看看它是否能给我们关于这个控制台可能是做什么的提示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

...The surfaces inside the dome glow with a faint yellow light. An unknown script covers the walls from top to bottom.\n\nAt the center of the main chamber is a console with two large buttons: one deep blue, the other a vibrant green. We are in the process of deciphering the text on the walls, to see if it gives us an indication of what this console might be for.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:279`

## astral_rift.1510.desc.psionic_or_latent

### 中文

$astral_rift.1510.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName]汇报了踏入这个穹顶的困难性。显然这里有什么怪异的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

$astral_rift.1510.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName] has reported uneasiness since stepping into the dome. Something is wrong here.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:281`

## astral_rift.1510.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1510.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1510.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName]在进入穹顶时汇报了传感器故障。建议谨慎行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

$astral_rift.1510.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName] has reported sensor abnormalities since entering the dome. Caution is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:282`

## astral_rift.1510.b.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1510.b.default&lang=en)

### 中文

究竟发生了什么？他们走丢了吗？

### English

What on [Explorer.GetHomeWorldName] is going on? Weren't they lost?

## astral_rift.1510.b.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1510.b.hive&lang=en)

这不符合逻辑，他们不是已经走丢了吗？

### English

This defies logic, were they not lost to us?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:284`

## astral_rift.1510.b.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1510.b.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

停止计算。他们走丢了。尽管如此，了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

Does not compute. They were lost. Nonetheless, acknowledged.

## astral_rift.1515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失去联系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Lost Contact

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:287`

## astral_rift.1515.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1515.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很遗憾地通报，我们彻底失去了与外派团队的联系。\n\n收到的最后一份报告是一段难以辨认的尖叫声，随后是一片寂静。\n\n试图重建通信的努力没有成功。\n\n我们还没有拉下紧急锚，希望这支队伍还能回来，但希望似乎有点渺茫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

We regret to report that we have lost contact with the away team.\n\nThe last report received was an audio log of indecipherable screaming followed by an unbroken silence.\n\nAttempts to reestablish communications have been unsuccessful.\n\nWe have not yet pulled the emergency anchor in the hopes that the team might yet return, but the outlook seems grim.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:288`

## astral_rift.1515.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1515.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们突然与[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]失去了联络。但我们暂时还没拉回紧急锚链，希望只是暂时失联。由于没有进入裂隙的办法，这就是我们唯一能做的了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

We have suddenly lost contact with our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]. We have not yet pulled the emergency anchor in the hopes that this disconnect is temporary. With no way to enter the rift, this is all we can do.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:289`

## astral_rift.1515.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1515.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们寄予最好的希望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

Let's hope for the best.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:290`

## astral_rift.1520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抉择

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

A Choice

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:292`

## astral_rift.1520.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1520.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地与一头雾水的科学团队进行了同步。\n\n他们对于发送两次汇报或是任何灾难都没有任何记忆。两粉重复报告的时间戳完全一致。也就是说这里的时间流动一定是受到了某种干扰。\n\n我们的科学家们被困住了。一个穹顶牢牢地罩在其上，与§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的沟通也仅限于无线电信号。\n\n通过计算这次汇报和之前的时间差，在3个小时的相对时间后这支外派队会到达那个尖叫重复的时刻。在这么短的时间内翻译墙上的文字不切实际，这支科研团队唯一可行的方案是按下控制台上的其中一个按钮，以期改善现在的状况。\n\n无论是§B蓝色§!或是§G绿色§!的按钮，它们的功能差异都没有任何提示。\n\n我们只能相信运气了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

We have synchronized with our very confused science team.\n\nThey have no memory of sending the report twice, or of any disaster. Timestamps for the duplicate reports are identical. There must be some disruptions in the flow of time here.\n\nOur scientists are trapped. The dome has sealed shut, and access to the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! is limited to radio contact only.\n\nCalculating the times between this report and the last, the away team has 3 hours of relative time before the screaming will be repeated. Translating the walls within this timeframe is impractical. The only course of action is for the science team to press one of the buttons on the console and hope it improves their situation.\n\nThere is no indication of the function for either the §Bblue§! or §Ggreen§! button.\n\nWe'll have to trust to luck.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:293`

## astral_rift.1520.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1520.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地与失联的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]重新建立了同步。\n\n这里出现了一些不一致的情况：他们不记得发送过两份报告，也不记得导致他们突然沉默的事件。\n\n计算这份报告和上次报告之间的时间戳，我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]在再次断开连接之前还有3小时的相对时间。在这个时间期限内翻译墙上的文字是不切实际的。虽然没有说明§B蓝色§!或者§G绿色§!按钮的功能，但唯一可行的是按下其中一个，希望它能改善情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

We have re-synchronized with our lost [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural].\n\nThere is a discrepancy: they do not have any recollection of sending two reports, nor can they remember the events that caused their sudden silence.\n\nCalculating the timestamps between this report and the last, our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural] have 3 hours of relative time before the disconnect is repeated. Translating the walls within this timeframe is impractical. Although there is no indication of the function of either the §Bblue§! or §Ggreen§! Buttons, the only course of action is to choose one in the hope that it improves the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:294`

按下§B蓝色§!按钮。

### English

Press the §Bblue§! button.

## astral_rift.1525.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1525.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学团队回来了，我们成功地建立了通讯。依据当地时，我们还有2小时来拯救他们。\n\n试过了§G绿色§!按钮之后，我们现在只剩下§B蓝色§!这个选项了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

Our science team is back, and we have reestablished communications. According to their local time, there are 2 hours left to save them.\n\nHaving tried the §Ggreen§! button last time, all that remains is to try the §Bblue§!.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:299`

## astral_rift.1525.desc.psionic_or_latent

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1525.desc.psionic_or_latent&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1525.desc.common$\n\n我们还没下达命令，[Leader.GetName]却告诉我们，[Leader.GetHeShe]在盯着§G绿色§!按钮的时候，被一种深深的恐惧感压垮。[Leader.GetHeSheCap]强烈建议还是试试§B蓝色§!的吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

$astral_rift.1525.desc.common$\n\nBefore we could issue this order, we were told by [Leader.GetName] that [Leader.GetHeShe] [Leader.GetIsAre] overwhelmed with a strong feeling of dread when [Leader.GetHeShe] glance[Leader.GetSXX] at the §Ggreen§! button. [Leader.GetHeSheCap] strongly recommend[Leader.GetSXX] trying the §Bblue§!.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:301`

## astral_rift.1525.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1525.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

$astral_rift.1525.desc.common$\n\nThe psionic processors of [Leader.GetName] have calculated that the optimal approach is to press the §Bblue§! button.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:302`

## astral_rift.1530.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1530.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第二密室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

The Second Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:305`

## astral_rift.1530.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1530.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得科研团队汇报他们按下了§B蓝色§!按钮，整个房间在一阵嘈杂的研磨声中共振了起来。一扇隐藏的门徐徐打开，露出了第二个房间。\n\n房间的墙壁发出淡淡的紫光，点亮了每一面墙上都布满着的无数象形文字。\n\n这里还有第二个控制台。和上一个一样，它也只有两个按钮。这一次，一个雕刻着等腰三角形，另一个则是正五边形。\n\n是时候做出又一个选择了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

Our science team reports that when they pressed the §Bblue§! button, the chamber resonated with a great grinding sound. A hidden door swung open, revealing a second chamber.\n\nThe walls of that chamber glowed a faint purple, lighting up a multitude of pictograms that cover every visible surface.\n\nThere is a second console. As with the last, it features exactly two buttons. This time, one has an engraving of an isosceles triangle, and the other a regular pentagon.\n\nIt is time to make another choice.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:306`

## astral_rift.1530.a

### English

## astral_rift.1535.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1535.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

一定是按错按钮了。\n\n然而，可以相信时间会再次循环，让外派团队做出不同的选择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

Must have been the wrong button.\n\nHowever, it is conceivable that time might loop again, allowing the away team to make a different choice.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:311`

## astral_rift.1535.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1535.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们又一次与外派团队失去了联系。他们最后的语音记录是更多的尖叫和火焰的噼啪声。\n\n$astral_rift.1535.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

Once again, we have lost contact with the away team. Their last recorded audio is more screaming and the crackle of flame.\n\n$astral_rift.1535.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:312`

## astral_rift.1535.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1535.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]被烧毁了。\n\n$astral_rift.1535.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

Our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural] have been incinerated.\n\n$astral_rift.1535.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:313`

## astral_rift.1535.a

## astral_rift.1540.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1540.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第二密室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

The Second Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:316`

## astral_rift.1540.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1540.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学团队回来了，我们成功地建立了通讯。依据当地时，我们还有[This.freeze_time_left_1540]小时来拯救他们。\n\n以之前的时间循环为鉴，科学团队安全地通过了第一个舱室。他们现在准备做出另一个抉择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

Our science team is back, and we have reestablished communications. According to their local time, there are [This.freeze_time_left_1540] hours left to save them.\n\nFollowing the lessons learned from the previous loops, the science team passed safely through the first chamber into the second. They are now prepared to make a different choice.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:317`

## astral_rift.1540.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1540.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1540.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

$astral_rift.1540.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:318`

## astral_rift.1540.desc.psionic_or_latent

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1540.desc.psionic_or_latent&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1540.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName]说我们一开始就该按五边形的，三角形可不是什么能带来好预兆的形状。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

$astral_rift.1540.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName] says that we should have known to try the pentagon from the start. The triangle has always been an ill omen.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:319`

## astral_rift.1540.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1540.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1540.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName]的灵能处理器显示，在面对这样的选择时最优举动总是正五边形。三角形是一个相对欠佳的模式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

$astral_rift.1540.desc.common$\n\nThe psionic processors of [Leader.GetName] indicate that the best course of action when presented with such a choice has always been the pentagon. The triangle is a comparatively less interesting pattern.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:320`

$astral_rift.1530.b$

### English

$astral_rift.1530.b$

## astral_rift.1545.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1545.name&lang=en)

### 中文

错误按钮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

Wrong Button

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:323`

## astral_rift.1545.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1545.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

按错按钮了。\n\n团队几乎没有时间发送一份简短的报告，他们的通信就被切断了。按下三角形按钮会使密室内充满腐蚀性气体。\n\n如果他们再次回到过去，我们就有机会尝试另一个按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

Wrong button.\n\nThe team barely had time to send a short report before their communication was cut. Pressing the triangle button caused the chamber to fill with a corrosive gas.\n\nIf they loop back to their past once more, we will have an opportunity to try the other button.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:324`

## astral_rift.1545.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1545.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

选择三角形让舱室充满了腐蚀性气体，一瞬间就把我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]溶解了。\n\n如果他们再次回到过去，我们就有机会尝试另一个按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

Choosing the triangle caused the chamber to fill with a corrosive gas that swiftly dissolved our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural].\n\nIf they loop back to their past once more, we will have an opportunity to try the other button.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:325`

## astral_rift.1545.desc.toxoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1545.desc.toxoid&lang=en)

### 中文

选择三角形让舱室充满了腐蚀性气体。尽管我们许多的设备被破坏了，但科学小组坚韧的身体毫发无损。在确认了他们的安全后，他们给我们发了一条信息，说会再试试五边形按钮，然后再回来报告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

Choosing the triangle caused the chamber to fill with a corrosive gas. While much of our equipment was damaged, the sturdy constitution of the science team allowed them to survive unimpaired. After confirming their safety, they sent us a message saying they will try the pentagon button and report back.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:326`

## astral_rift.1545.desc.toxoid_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1545.desc.toxoid_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

选择三角形让舱室充满了腐蚀性气体。尽管我们许多的设备被破坏了，但[explorer.GetScientistPlural]坚韧的身体毫发无损。当气体被散去后他们就会再尝试另一个按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Choosing the triangle caused the chamber to fill with a corrosive gas. While much of our equipment was damaged, the sturdy constitution of the [explorer.GetScientistPlural] allowed them to survive unimpaired. Once the gas has cleared, they will try the other button.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:327`

## astral_rift.1545.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1545.b&lang=en)

### 中文

真走运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

Fortunate.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:328`

## astral_rift.1550.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1550.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第三密室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

The Third Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:330`

## astral_rift.1550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

按下五边形按钮后，地面发生了碎裂，露出一个深入地下的螺旋楼梯。\n\n楼梯底部是第三个房间。在柔和的蓝光照射下，墙壁完全裸露。和以前一样，这个房间主要是一个有两个按钮的单独控制台。一个上面有四个点，而另一个则有六个点。\n\n又到了做出抉择的时候了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Pressing the pentagon caused the floor to rupture, revealing a spiraling staircase that descends deep underground.\n\nAt the bottom was a third chamber. Illuminated in a soft blue glow, the walls are completely bare. As before, this room is dominated by a solitary console with two buttons. One depicts four dots, while the other shows six.\n\nIt's time to make another choice.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:331`

## astral_rift.1550.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1550.a&lang=en)

### 中文

按下四个点的按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Press button with four dots.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:332`

## astral_rift.1550.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1550.b&lang=en)

### 中文

按下六个点的按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Press button with six dots.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:333`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

Our science team is back, and we have reestablished communications. According to their local time, there are [This.freeze_time_left_1555] hours left to save them.

## astral_rift.1555.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1555.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

……按照我们之前循环的经验，我们顺利通过了第一个和第二个密室。现在希望六点的按钮相比另一个会不那么致命一点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

...Following the directions of our previous loops we passed through the first and second chamber without issue. Hopefully the hexa-dotted button is less deadly than its counterpart.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:338`

## astral_rift.1555.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1555.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

……按照之前循环的经验，我们顺利通过了第一个和第二个密室。基于预测数据，六点按钮在统计上比其它的更安全一些。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

...Following the directions of our previous loops we passed through the first and second chamber without issue. Based on predictive data, the hexa-dotted button may be statistically less dangerous than its counterpart.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:339`

## astral_rift.1555.desc.psionic_or_latent

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1555.desc.psionic_or_latent&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]自信地带领着团队通过了第一和第二个密室。他们在没有我们的命令之下就做出了正确的选择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

[Leader.GetName] has led the team confidently back through the first and second chambers. They have chosen the correct buttons without needing our commands.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:340`

## astral_rift.1555.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1555.desc.psionic_or_latent_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]高效地带领着团队通过了第一和第二个密室，他们依靠灵能处理器的引导在没有我们的命令之下就做出了正确的选择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

[Leader.GetName] has efficiently led the team back through the first and second chambers, their psionic processors guiding them to the correct outcome independent of our commands.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:341`

## astral_rift.1560.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1560.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

按钮错误。\n\n[Leader.GetName]的最后遗言表明，按下四点按钮让密室里充满了水。\n\n如果他们再次回到过去，我们就有机会尝试另一个按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Wrong button.\n\nThe audio recording of [Leader.GetName]'s final words indicated that the button with four dots filled the chamber with a deluge of water.\n\nIf they loop back to their past once more, we will have an opportunity to try the other button.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:355`

## astral_rift.1560.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1560.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

按钮错误。\n\n四点按钮会让密室被水淹没。我们的[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]死了。\n\n如果他们再次回到过去，我们就有机会尝试另一个按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Wrong button.\n\nThe button with four dots filled the chamber with a deluge of water. Our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural] are no more.\n\nIf they loop back to their past once more, we will have an opportunity to try the other button.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:356`

## astral_rift.1560.desc.aquatic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1560.desc.aquatic&lang=en)

### 中文

按下四点的按钮让舱室充满了大量的水。幸好我们的[Leader.GetName]深识水性，这简直跟回到家一样。他们打算等水退去以后再按下另一个按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Pressing the button with four dots filled the chamber with a great swell of water. Fortunately, due to the aquatic nature of [Leader.GetName], this was barely an inconvenience. They intend to press the other button once the water has been fully drained.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:357`

## astral_rift.1565.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1565.name&lang=en)

### 中文

时间水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Time Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:361`

## astral_rift.1565.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1565.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

一个装有巨大水晶的底座拔地而起。建筑里所有的门都打开了。\n\n当精密计时器靠近晶体时，在极端值之间剧烈振荡。这一定是时间循环的来源，也是这栋建筑的关键部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

A pedestal with a giant crystal has risen from the ground. All the doors in the complex have opened.\n\nChronometers oscillate wildly between extreme values when in close proximity to the crystal. This must be the source of the time loops, and a key part of this building's purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:362`

## astral_rift.1565.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1565.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1565.desc.common$\n\n在这个房间里的选择似乎完全是随意设置的。也许它们的目的是测试在循环之间传递信息的能力?\n\n不管怎样，活下来我们就能带着水晶离开了。它影响时间流动的能力应该会极其有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

$astral_rift.1565.desc.common$\n\nThe choices within this gauntlet of chambers seemed entirely arbitrary. Perhaps they were meant to test the ability to pass information between loops?\n\nEither way, surviving it allows us to leave with the crystal. Its ability to affect the flow of time could prove extremely useful.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:363`

## astral_rift.1565.desc.latent_psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1565.desc.latent_psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1555.desc.common$\n\n这次经验改变了[Leader.GetName]。似乎[Leader.GetHisHer]的这趟时间循环经历使[Leader.GetHisHer]灵能潜力发生了完全觉醒！\n\n也许这就是这座废墟的最终目的，一系列看似随意的选择只能由跨越时间线的力量所引导，例如灵能。\n\n无论如何，我们现在都安全地带着晶体离开了。它影响时间流动的能力应该会极其有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

$astral_rift.1555.desc.common$\n\nThis experience has changed [Leader.GetName]. It seems that [Leader.GetHisHer] experience during the time loops has awakened [Leader.GetHisHer] latent psionic potential!\n\nPerhaps this was the ultimate purpose of the ruin, a set of arbitrary choices that can only be navigated with a power that transcends time, such as psionic intuition.\n\nEither way, we can now safely leave with the crystal. Its ability to affect the flow of time could prove to be very useful.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:364`

## astral_rift.1565.desc.latent_psionic_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1565.desc.latent_psionic_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1555.desc.common$\n\n休眠在[Leader.GetName]中的灵能处理器在经历时间循环之后被唤醒了，这种环境为这种能力的上线提供了严苛的测试条件。\n\n现在还不确定是否就是这座废墟的原本目的，但无论如何我们现在已经可以安全地带着水晶离开，进行进一步的时间分析了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

$astral_rift.1555.desc.common$\n\nExposure to these time loops has awakened the dormant psionic processor units within [Leader.GetName], the environment providing a solid test bed to bring these powers online.\n\nIt is unclear if this was the original purpose of these ruins. Regardless, we are now able to safely leave with the crystal for further chronological analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:365`

## astral_rift.1565.desc.psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1565.desc.psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1565.desc.common$\n\n在这些轮回中，[Leader.GetName]的直觉帮助[Leader.GetHimHer]做出了正确的抉择。\n\n也许这就是这些密室的目的，设计一系列看似随意的选择来揭开灵能潜力。\n\n不管怎样，我们现在可以安全地带着晶体离开了。它影响时间流动的能力应该会极其有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

$astral_rift.1565.desc.common$\n\nThroughout these loops, [Leader.GetName]'s intuition helped [Leader.GetHimHer] to make the correct choice.\n\nPerhaps this was the point of these chambers, a set of arbitrary choices designed to uncover psionic potential.\n\nEither way, we can now safely leave with the crystal. Its ability to affect the flow of time could prove to be very useful.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:366`

## astral_rift.1565.desc.psionic_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1565.desc.psionic_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1565.desc.common$\n\n[Leader.GetName]在灵能处理器的帮助下得到了最优的结果，穿过了这些轮回。\n\n也许这就是这些密室的目的，设计一系列看似随意的选择来揭开灵能潜力。\n\n不管怎样，我们现在可以安全地带着晶体离开做进一步的时间分析了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

$astral_rift.1565.desc.common$\n\nThroughout these loops, [Leader.GetName]'s psionic processors helped lead them to the optimal outcome.\n\nPerhaps this was the point of these chambers, a set of arbitrary choices designed to uncover psionic potential.\n\nRegardless, we are now able to safely leave with the crystal for further chronological analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:367`

## personality_mirrored_empire_default_desc

## personality_mirrored_empire_spiritualist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apersonality_mirrored_empire_spiritualist_desc&lang=en)

## fractured_ambassadors_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afractured_ambassadors_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在星界位面里遇见的镜像集体处于一种碎片化的意识中，但可以帮助我们与其他相似生命形式进行协商。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Our mirror collective we encountered beyond the astral planes is of a fractured mind, but has offered to help us in our negotiations with other similar lifeforms.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:400`

## astral_rift.1600.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.name&lang=en)

### 中文

镜中影

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Through the Mirror

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:405`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

在碎片的另一头，我们发现了一座与我们自己恒星基地相似的结构。我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!刚一接近，就收到了许多语言和许多频段的问候。令人惊讶的是，这是我们自己的语言。\n\n招待我们人与我们一样惊讶，因为我们居然也在说着他们的语言。但在简短礼貌的交谈后，我们发现这块星域是一个与我们颇为相似的宇航文明。他们自称为[mirrored_empire.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

Beyond the rift, we discovered a structure that resembled one of our own starbases. As soon as our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! approached, we were hailed in a number of languages and on several frequencies. Surprisingly, one of those languages was our own.\n\nOur hosts were just as shocked as we were to encounter aliens speaking their own tongue. But after a brief exchange of courtesies, we learned that this sector is home to a space-faring civilization much like our own. They call it the [mirrored_empire.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:406`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

这些人们混杂着熟悉感和陌生感，令人有些不安。我们也许可以与他们合作，但我们能相信这些镜像人吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

These people are a disturbing mix of the familiar and the strange. It might be possible to work with them, but can we trust these doppelgangers?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:407`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.authoritarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.authoritarian&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们觉得社会的需求远高于个人欲望，他们则默许自私大众的混沌行为。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we understand that the needs of society outweigh the needs of an individual, they allow the selfish masses to follow a path of chaos.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:408`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.egalitarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.egalitarian&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们知道社会公平是欣欣向荣的基础，而他们则允许国家掌控大众生活的方方面面。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we know that social equality is the foundation of a thriving society, they allow the state to control their people's lives.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:409`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们已经抛弃了迷信，但他们仍保有天真的信仰体系。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we have cast off superstition, they still cling to their naive belief systems.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:410`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们已经看破红尘，而他们却还是执迷不悟。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we see through this material world, they remain locked in illusion.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:411`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.militarist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们理解勇气和纪律的宝贵，而他们却认为英勇孔武已经是过去式了。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we understand the virtues of courage and discipline, they view martial valor as archaic.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:412`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们我们已经摒弃了战争，而他们还沉浸在军事霸权的破坏性纪律中。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we have moved beyond war, they still cling to the destructive principles of military supremacy.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:413`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们寻求合作，而他们则将自己与整个星系隔绝开。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we seek collaboration, they isolate themselves from the rest of the galaxy.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:414`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$他们与我们有许多相似的理念，除了一点，我们以自己的人民为优先，而他们却对亲近外星文明有着离谱的幼稚。\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

$astral_rift.1600.desc.1$ They share many of our ideals, but where we put our people first, they embrace alien civilizations with a surprising naivety.\n\n$astral_rift.1600.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:415`

## astral_rift.1600.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!刚一抵达恒星基地，就收到了许多语言在不同频段上的问候。\n\n这座空间站的拥有者只在一瞬间就翻译了我们的语言，并且邀请我们登站互通有无。\n\n我们这才发现这片星域是一个主要由[mirrored_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]构成的文明的家园，他们自称为[mirrored_empire.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

As soon as our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! arrived at the starbase, we were hailed in a number of languages and on several frequencies.\n\nThe owners of the station successfully translated our language with remarkable speed and welcomed us aboard to exchange information.\n\nWe have since discovered that this sector is home to a civilization primarily composed of [mirrored_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] that calls itself the [mirrored_empire.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:416`

## astral_rift.1600.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.a&lang=en)

研究他们的人民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Study their people.

## astral_rift.1600.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1600.b&lang=en)

研究他们的舰队。

### English

Study their fleets.

## astral_rift.1605.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.name&lang=en)

### 中文

历史课

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

History Lesson

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:420`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

过去几个月，我们都在与[mirrored_empire.GetName]进行文化交换。\n他们的历史与我们在超光速科技发现为止如出一辙，但他们后来遭遇了一艘无比强大的未知外星舰船。这艘舰船目中无人地继续航行，没人知道它的目的，但这次遭遇深深撼动了他们的社会的根基。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

We have spent the last month in a cultural exchange with the [mirrored_empire.GetName].\nTheir society mirrored our own until briefly before FTL technology was discovered, when they encountered an unknown alien vessel of unimaginable power. The vessel moved on without its purpose ever being discovered, but the brief visit shocked their society to the core.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:421`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

对我们而言，他们显然做出了错误的选择，但这也给我们提出了一个有趣的问题。如果我们的社会也在那么早期遭遇到相同的事情，我们也会走上那样错误的道路吗？\n\n他们很不幸运地在影响重大的前超光速阶段遭遇这样的事。他们基础思潮的改变大大拖慢了帝国的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

It is clear to us that they made the incorrect choice, but it does pose an interesting question. Would our society also have gone down the wrong path had the same thing happened to us so early on?\n\nIt is unfortunate for them that this event happened during the impressionable pre-FTL period of their history. Changing their foundational ethics undoubtedly slowed the progress of their empire.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:422`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:423`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.authoritarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.authoritarian&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在技术先进的访客面前如此无助，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，倒向了平等主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against these technologically advanced visitors lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards egalitarianism.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:424`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.egalitarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.egalitarian&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在如此无助地想对抗如此技术先进的舰船，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，他们在准备应对未来遭遇的过程中倒向了威权主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against this craft lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards authoritarianism as they sought to prepare against any further encounters.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:425`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在对抗这艘舰船时如此无助，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，倒向了唯心主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against this craft lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards spiritualism.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:426`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在对抗这艘舰船时如此无助，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，倒向了唯物主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against this craft lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards materialism.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:427`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.militarist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在对抗这艘舰船时如此无助，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，他们认为在未来的遭遇中应采取和平的方案，从而倒向了和平主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against this craft lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards pacifism as they sought a peaceful resolution to any further encounters.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:428`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在对抗这艘舰船时如此无助，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，他们觉得要为未来遭遇做好准备，从而倒向了军国主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against this craft lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards militarism as they sought to prepare for any further encounters.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:429`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在对抗这艘舰船时如此无助，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，他们觉得要为未来的遭遇做好准备，从而倒向了排外主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against this craft lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards xenophobia as they sought to prepare for any further encounters.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:430`

## astral_rift.1605.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$看到他们在对抗这艘舰船时如此无助，给他们的意识形态带去了翻天覆地的变化，他们认为在未来的遭遇中应采取和平的方案，从而倒向了亲外主义。\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

$astral_rift.1605.desc.1$ Seeing how helpless they would have been against this craft lead to an upheaval in their ideology and a shift towards xenophilia as they sought a peaceful resolution to any further encounters.\n\n$astral_rift.1605.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:431`

## astral_rift.1605.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1605.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的不幸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

Unfortunate for them.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:432`

## astral_rift.1610.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1610.name&lang=en)

### 中文

研究他们的舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Studying Their Fleets

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:434`

## astral_rift.1610.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1610.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在表面上我们与[mirrored_empire.GetName]进行着信息文化的交换，背地里悄悄地调查他们的军事能力。\n\n[mirrored_empire.GetName]的计算机使用与我们相似的加密系统，进入他们的数据库易如反掌。\n\n这个文明的军事科技与我们大致相同，但也存在足够大的差异，让他们的舰船图解对工程师们而言已经足够有趣了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

While on the surface we engaged in a cultural exchange of information with the [mirrored_empire.GetName], behind the scenes we quietly investigated their military capabilities.\n\n[mirrored_empire.GetName]'s computers use a similar encryption to our own, which made accessing their databases extremely easy.\n\nThis civilization's military technology is similar to our own, but there are enough differences that the schemata of their vessels should prove interesting to our engineers.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:435`

## astral_rift.1610.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1610.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们高度相似，但是很难相信他们。$astral_rift.1610.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

Despite our many similarities, it is hard to trust them. $astral_rift.1610.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:436`

## astral_rift.1611.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1611.name&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎思维

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Fractured Mind

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:447`

## astral_rift.1611.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1611.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们花费了过去的一周与[mirrored_empire.GetName]进行了文化交换。\n\n我们的兴趣很快变成了警觉，因为发现这些生物是破坏集体意识的残余物。在他们文明发展的某个时间点上，他们的意识被个体概念荼毒了。他们有目的地割裂自身，慢慢地每个自个体都不再听从集体，只忠于自己了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

We have spent the last week in a cultural exchange with the [mirrored_empire.GetName].\n\nOur interest quickly turned to alarm as we discovered that these beings are what remains of a broken collective consciousness. At some point in their civilization's development, their mind became poisoned with the concept of individuality. Purposefully segmenting itself over time, eventually each drone began to hear no thoughts but their own.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:448`

## astral_rift.1615.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外交机遇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Diplomatic Opportunity

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:449`

## astral_rift.1615.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎我们在空间站的存在激起了他们指挥链的警觉。一位[mirrored_empire.GetName]特使被派到恒星基地以讨论我们人民的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

It seems knowledge of our presence on the station has escalated up their chain of command. An emissary of the [mirrored_empire.GetName] has been assigned to the starbase to discuss the future of our peoples.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:450`

## astral_rift.1615.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要怎么回应呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

How do we wish to respond?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:451`

## astral_rift.1615.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common$\n\n他们不但排斥我们，而且不理解我们。他们辩称两边人民的协调一致对双方都好。\n\n$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common$\n\nWhile they do not like us, they do understand us. They argue that an arrangement between our people would benefit both sides.\n\n$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:452`

## astral_rift.1615.desc.common.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.desc.common.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common$\n\n他们热情洋溢地阐述了我们的相似之处远大于不同之处，并辩称两边人民的协调一致对双方都好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common$\n\nThey have made an impassioned case that our similarities far outweigh our differences. They argue that an arrangement between our people would benefit both sides.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:453`

## astral_rift.1615.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.gestalt$\n\n我们确实承袭了很多共同点。$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.gestalt$\n\nIt is true that we share a unique heritage together. $astral_rift.1615.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:454`

## astral_rift.1615.desc.common.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.desc.common.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common$\n\n他们热情洋溢地阐述了我们除了名字不同以外就是一样的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common$\n\nThey have made an impassioned case that we are one people in all but name.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:455`

## astral_rift.1615.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1615.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.default$在一个充满了未知力量的星系中，我们之间的友谊毫无疑问会为双方都带去好处的。\n\n$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.default$ Surely in a galaxy full of unknown powers, a friendship between us would serve to benefit both parties.\n\n$astral_rift.1615.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:456`

### 中文

这场闹剧已经进行得够久了。从他们那里我们已经拿走了能拿的一切，对跨次元的进一步外交也兴趣平平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

## astral_rift.1620.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1620.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新友谊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

New Friendship

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:468`

## astral_rift.1620.desc.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1620.desc.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们给予大使积极正面的回应。\n\n促进两个宇宙间的长期沟通渠道正在建立起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

We have given the emissary a favorable response.\n\nWork is being done to facilitate a more permanent pathway between our two universes.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:469`

## astral_rift.1620.desc.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1620.desc.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管还没有确定任何事情，大使指出，[final_mirrored_empire.GetName]会对贸易协定更感兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Although nothing is decided yet, the emissary has indicated that [final_mirrored_empire.GetName] would be interested in trade agreements.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:470`

## astral_rift.1620.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1620.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1620.desc.common_1$$astral_rift.1620.desc.common_2$\n\n我们很期待与我们能视为表亲的人们通力协作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

$astral_rift.1620.desc.common_1$ $astral_rift.1620.desc.common_2$\n\nWe look forward to working with a people that we consider to be our close cousins.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:471`

## astral_rift.1620.desc.xeno

## astral_rift.1620.desc.gestalt

### 中文

$astral_rift.1620.desc.common_1$他们分散的思维虽然效率不及我们，但可以在我们与其他的分散个体群之间充当“润滑剂”的功能。

## astral_rift.1625.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1625.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们给予大使负面的回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

We have given the emissary a negative response.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:476`

## astral_rift.1625.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1625.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1625.desc.common$\n\n尽管[final_mirrored_empire.GetName]对所谓的相似点和共同未来夸夸其谈，但是对能为我们人民带来繁荣的基本理念嗤之以鼻。\n\n撂下我们的话之后，在他们对我们的外交豁免权达到极限之前，我们迅速回到了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!上。\n\n尽管我们无意与这群人保持联络，但研究两个社会间的差异让我们对自己的理解又更加深入了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

$astral_rift.1625.desc.common$\n\nFor all their talk of similarities and a shared future, [final_mirrored_empire.GetName] turned their back on the fundamental ideals that make our people great.\n\nHaving said our piece, we swiftly returned to our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! before they decided that our diplomatic immunity had reached its limits.\n\nAlthough we have no plans to maintain contact with these people, studying the differences between this civilization and our own has taught us a great deal about ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:477`

## astral_rift.1625.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1625.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1625.desc.common$\n\n尽管他们对所谓相似之处夸夸其谈，但我们觉得没有什么事比抛弃集体更加可恶了。\n\n在阐明了我们的立场之后，[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]迅速地返回了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!以免事态发生什么意料之外的变化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

$astral_rift.1625.desc.common$\n\nFor all their talk of similarities, we can think of nothing more abhorrent than abandoning the collective.\n\nHaving made our stance clear, our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural] swiftly returned to the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! before the situation took a more dangerous turn.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:478`

## astral_rift.1625.desc.common.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1625.desc.common.homicidal&lang=en)

### English

They are lucky their civilization is so far out of reach.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:479`

## astral_rift.1700.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1700.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮奥秘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

Floating Mystery

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:493`

## astral_rift.1700.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1700.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里既没有恒星，也没有行星。只有一束来自神秘来源的漫射光照亮了这个巨大的物体，在我们的面前若隐若现。\n\n它的形状大致为矩形，处于完全沉默的状态——没有可探测到的推进系统或任何形式的动力特征。它的大小与一个小型恒星基地相当，看起来死气沉沉。没人知道它在这个隐蔽的口袋维度上漂浮了多久，或是从何而来。\n\n无数的装饰字形和符号被复杂地雕刻在它的表面。由此我们排除了这是自然现象的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

There are no stars here, no planetary bodies. Only a diffuse light of mysterious origin reveals the massive object looming before us.\n\nRoughly rectangular in shape, it is completely inert - there are no detectable propulsion systems or power signatures of any kind. Comparable in size to a small starbase, it appears dead. For how long has it been floating in this secluded pocket dimension, and where did it come from?\n\nInnumerable decorative glyphs and symbols have been intricately carved into its surface. We have ruled out any possibility of this being a natural phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:494`

## astral_rift.1700.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1700.a&lang=en)

### 中文

试着翻译文字。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

Attempt to translate the glyphs.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:495`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1700.b&lang=en)

扫描它的整个外部。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

Scan its entire perimeter.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:496`

## astral_rift.1705.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1705.name&lang=en)

### 中文

千言万语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

Millions of Glyphs

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:498`

## astral_rift.1705.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1705.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

覆盖在这个巨大物体上的象形文字大多是以形表意的。\n\n根据同位素定年法我们确定，最早的一些雕刻比最新的要早上几千年。这些象形文字的栩栩如生，因此我们很快就能做出一个粗略的翻译。\n\n这个物体实际上是一座巨大的墓室，埋葬其中的人曾经统治过整个星系。最早的一些象形文字揭示了这位强大生物的起源。\n\n有两个物种在太空星神兽种族的抚养下长大，并发展出了一种独特的共生关系。他们结成了强大的灵能纽带，一起征服了无数的星系。\n\n还有另一些符号似乎是某种“诅咒”。试图打开这个物体——甚至太靠近它——都可能是危险的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

The glyphs that cover this tremendous object are mostly pictorial representations.\n\nBased on the carbon dating, we have determined that some of the earliest carvings were made several millennia before the most recent. The glyphs are illustrated in such detail, that we were soon able to develop a rough translation.\n\nThis object is in fact a massive burial chamber. Whoever, or whatever, is entombed inside once ruled an entire galaxy. Some of the earliest pictographs reveal the origin of this powerful being.\n\nRaised by a space-born race of leviathans, the two species developed a unique symbiosis. Forming a potent psionic bond, they conquered countless systems together.\n\nThere are other symbols which refer to a "curse". Attempting to open the object - or even approaching it too closely - may be dangerous.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:499`

扫描它的整个外部。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Scan its entire perimeter.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:500`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1705.b&lang=en)
