把它炸开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

Blast it open.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:501`

## astral_rift.1710.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1710.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古封印

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

An Ancient Seal

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:503`

## astral_rift.1710.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1710.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在完全已经进入了环绕该物体的轨道。\n\n它由两半焊接而成。在一些地方，焊缝被严重腐蚀——考虑到周围并没有什么大气，这还挺出乎意料的。这也表明腐蚀的来源一定来自于物体本身的内部。\n\n腐蚀非常严重，甚至在一些地方出现了裂缝，大到足以让我们的一架无人机进入。\n\n或者，我们也可以用携带的炸药炸开封印。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

We have completed a full orbit around the object.\n\nIt is composed of two halves welded shut. The seal is heavily corroded in some locations - unexpected given the emptiness of the surrounding atmosphere. This indicates that the source of the corrosion must be coming from inside the object itself.\n\nThe corrosion is so severe that in some places cracks have appeared, large enough for one of our drones to enter.\n\nAlternatively, we are carrying enough explosives to crack the seal.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:504`

## astral_rift.1710.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1710.a&lang=en)

### 中文

通过裂缝派出一架无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

Send a drone through the crack.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:505`

## astral_rift.1710.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1710.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把它炸开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

Blast it open.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:506`

## astral_rift.1715.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1715.name&lang=en)

### 中文

云雾缭绕的通道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

Clouded Passageways

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:508`

## astral_rift.1715.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1715.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的无人机从物体内部发回了一份报告。\n\n不幸的是，大部分舱室都被厚厚的碳尘云遮住了，很难在其中闪转腾挪。\n\n初步分析显示大量的星界纤维和碳粒子交织在一起。如果我们肯花些时间应该是有可能收获这些材料的。\n\n透过远在结构深处的裂缝，我们发现了存在一个内室的迹象。如果想要进一步探索可能需要更破坏性的操作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Our drone has transmitted a report from inside the object.\n\nUnfortunately, most of the compartments within are obscured by a thick cloud of carbon dust and have proven difficult to maneuver around.\n\nInitial analysis has revealed a high amount of astral fabric interwoven with the carbon particles. It should be possible to harvest this material, should we take the time to do so.\n\nVisible through further cracks deep within the structure, we can see the signs of an inner chamber. Exploring further would require more destructive exploration.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:509`

## astral_rift.1715.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1715.a&lang=en)

### 中文

取一些样本就回来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Take samples and return.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:510`

## astral_rift.1715.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1715.b&lang=en)

### 中文

进行破坏性破拆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

Engage destructive exploration.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:511`

## astral_rift.1720.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.name&lang=en)

### 中文

盗墓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

Tomb Raiding

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:513`

## astral_rift.1720.desc.outside

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.desc.outside&lang=en)

### 中文

用标准的爆破药，我们在这个物体上成功地开出了一个不小的洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

Using standard excavational charges, we have successfully blasted a sizable hole into the object.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:514`

## astral_rift.1720.desc.drones

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.desc.drones&lang=en)

### 中文

用标准的爆破药，我们在这个物体的内部成功地开出了一条通路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Using standard excavational charges, we have successfully blasted open the passageways inside of the object.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:515`

## astral_rift.1720.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

它的内部装满了各种极具价值的文物。稀有合金铸成的复杂雕塑伫立在周围。内室的中这样是一具巨大的水晶骨架，其额头上装饰着精美的宝石。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

The interior is rich with valuable artifacts. Sophisticated statues made of rare alloys are arranged in ceremonial fashion. The center of the inner chamber is occupied by an enormous crystalline skeleton. Upon its brow lies a rich adornment of delicate gems.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:516`

## astral_rift.1720.desc.no_glyphs

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.desc.no_glyphs&lang=en)

### 中文

现在可以肯定地说，这整座建筑就是以坟墓设计建造的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

It is safe to assume that this entire structure was built as a kind of tomb.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:517`

## astral_rift.1720.desc.glyphs

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.desc.glyphs&lang=en)

### 中文

依据覆盖着这座纪念碑外围的文字记载，骨架周围所有的物体都是贡品。尽管他是一个无情的征服者，但他所留下的这个文明显然对其无比敬重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

As recorded in the glyphs covering the outside of this monument, all of the objects surrounding the skeleton were placed here as tribute. Though a ruthless conqueror, the civilization they left behind clearly held this being in great esteem.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:518`

## astral_rift.1720.desc.psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.desc.psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

装饰着他眉弓的每颗宝石都有一种潜在的灵能共振。这一定是这位巨人和它的共生体交流的方式。我们可以从这些设备中学到很多东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

Each of the gemstones crowning their brow pulse with a latent psionic resonance. This must be how this titan communicated with its symbionts. We can learn much from these devices.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:519`

## astral_rift.1720.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1720.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管这具遗骸尺寸过大无法从裂隙中带出，但我们可以带走最有价值的一些文物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

Though the skeleton is much too large to extract from the rift, we will be taking back the most valuable of the artifacts.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:520`

## astral_rift.1725.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1725.name&lang=en)

### 中文

墓地守卫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

Grave Guardians

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:528`

## astral_rift.1725.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1725.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的定向爆破打开了一条通向我们之前未发现墓室的通路。这个空间深藏于封印之后，显然做了一些反盗墓的设计。它里面还充满了敌对的实体。\n\n球体立刻拉动了锚链，但还是有许多守卫跟随着我们的尾流冲出了裂隙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

Our controlled explosion has opened the way to a previously undetected cavity. Hidden behind the seal, this space was clearly designed to counter intrusion. It was filled to the brim with hostile entities.\n\nThe Sphere immediately retrieved the anchor, but many of the guardians have exited the rift with us and are following in our wake.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:529`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1725.a&lang=en)

现在我们有些麻烦要处理了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

We have a problem to deal with.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:530`

## decision_breed_rockworm_hive_desc

### 中文

从星界裂隙另一头带回的年幼岩蠹，经过精心的训练后能挖出大量矿物通道。这能出什么乱子？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

The young Rockworms recovered from beyond the Astral Rift can be carefully trained to carve extensive mining tunnels. What could go wrong?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:538`

## astral_rift.1800.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1800.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无尽岩海

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

Endless Rock

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:543`

## astral_rift.1800.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1800.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一大片松散的岩砾在我们面前铺展开去，上方是一片苍白、空旷的天空。\n\n这里看不到行星或恒星。似乎不是星系或任何已知系统的一部分。在我们的传感器所能探测到的范围内，这片景观向各个方向无限延伸。地表布满了像小行星一样大的坑洞。\n\n在地平线的远方，几十头巨大的蠕虫状生物正从地面冒出来，用一对有力的鳍在稠密的大气中滑行。它们一定在地下有某种聚居地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

A vast plane of loose rock and gravel stretches before us. Above it hangs a pale, empty sky.\n\nNo planets or stars can be seen here. This does not appear to be part of a galaxy or any known system. As far as our sensors can detect, this landscape continues infinitely in all directions. The surface is pockmarked with holes as large as some asteroids.\n\nAll across the horizon, dozens of massive, worm-like creatures are bursting from the ground. Pairs of powerful fins allow them to glide through the dense atmosphere. They must have some type of colony underground.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:544`

## astral_rift.1800.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1800.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续地表观测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Continue surface observations.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:545`

## astral_rift.1800.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1800.b&lang=en)

### 中文

探索隧道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

Explore the tunnels.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:546`

## astral_rift.1805.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1805.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天空微生物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

Sky Plankton

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:548`

## astral_rift.1805.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1805.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里也不只有蠕虫存在。\n\n一种微小生物的群体在下层大气中形成了云雾般的结构。它们以一整个大群共同行动，在天空中形成了复杂的图案。\n\n那些巨大的蠕虫从隧道中破土而出，然后就向天空跃起滑翔，大口吞噬这些小生命构成的云。我们还观察到了许多蠕虫互相协作，把这些虫群赶到一起的情形。它们的合作程度颇为复杂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

There are more than the worms living here.\n\nSwarms of microscopic creatures form clouds in the lower atmosphere. Moving as one mass, they create complex patterns across the sky.\n\nAs the massive worms breach from their tunnels, they glide in the air, grazing among the living clouds. We have observed several worms working together to flush these swarms towards one another. The level of cooperation is fairly sophisticated.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:549`

## astral_rift.1805.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1805.a&lang=en)

### 中文

取样微生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Sample the microorganisms.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:550`

## astral_rift.1805.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1805.b&lang=en)

### 中文

探索蠕虫的隧道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

Explore the worms' tunnels.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:551`

## astral_rift.1810.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1810.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隧道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Tunnels

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:553`

## astral_rift.1810.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1810.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

地表下有一座由一系列复杂隧道互相交联的巨大洞穴网络。其中有几个坍塌的部分似乎没有被蠕虫使用。我们驾驶着§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!进入了最安静的区域。\n\n这里的岩壁上覆盖着一层网状物质，将页岩和松散的砾石固定在原地。我们推测这些是蠕虫的分泌物。\n\n大多数洞穴都是空的，但有些洞穴里有浑浊的水池。在其中一间岩室里，我们发现了一堆巨大的矿化茧半浸在水里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Beneath the surface lies a vast network of chambers connected by a complex series of tunnels. Several of these were partially collapsed, and appeared not to be in use by the worms. We piloted the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! into the quietest.\n\nThe walls are covered in a web-like substance which keeps the shale and loose gravel in place. We speculate that these are secretions from the worms.\n\nMost of the chambers are empty, but some contain murky pools of water. Within one, we encountered a clutch of large, mineralized cocoons half-submerged in the liquid.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:554`

## astral_rift.1810.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1810.a&lang=en)

拿走这些卵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

## astral_rift.1815.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1815.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生吞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

Swallowed

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:558`

## astral_rift.1815.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1815.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

情况一切正常。\n\n除了我们被吃了以外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

Situation normal.\n\nHowever, we have been eaten.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:559`

## astral_rift.1815.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1815.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!被困在了它的消化系统中。我们不知道这是一种敌意行为或仅仅是在进食。\n\n蠕虫分泌的消化液并不强烈，但我们也不能无限期地停留。我们可以通过拉动锚链来让自己从蠕虫口中的缝隙逃出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! is currently encased in its digestive system. We don't know if this was an aggressive act, or if the worm was simply grazing.\n\nThe digestive juices secreted by the worm are mild, but we cannot remain indefinitely. By pulling anchor, we could force ourselves through the gap in the worm's mouth.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:560`

## astral_rift.1815.desc.common.gas

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1815.desc.common.gas&lang=en)

### 中文

或者，蠕虫的内部看起来相当柔软，应该能用炸药炸开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

Alternatively, the worm's insides appear relatively soft, and should be susceptible to an explosion.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:561`

## astral_rift.1815.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1815.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1815.desc.common.1$\n\n我们在收集微生物云的样本时，一只正在觅食的蠕虫把我们整个鲸吞而下。\n\n$astral_rift.1815.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

$astral_rift.1815.desc.common.1$\n\nWhile we were engaged in collecting cloud samples, one of the grazing worms has engulfed us whole.\n\n$astral_rift.1815.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:563`

## astral_rift.1815.desc.gas

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1815.desc.gas&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1815.desc$\n\n$astral_rift.1815.desc.common.gas$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

$astral_rift.1815.desc$\n\n$astral_rift.1815.desc.common.gas$

## astral_rift.1820.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1820.name&lang=en)

### 中文

育儿室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

The Nursery

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:576`

## astral_rift.1820.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1820.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在隧道系统的深处发现了一处浅浅的湖泊。\n\n这里的墙体蠕动着。和我们§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!大小相当的幼虫正忙着啃噬那些用于把岩壁连接在一起的石化分泌物。虽然体型要小得多，但几乎可以肯定，它们是我们看到的在地面上滑翔的蠕虫的幼体。\n\n这里应该是某种育儿室。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:478`

### English

We have discovered a shallow lake deep within the tunnel system.\n\nThe walls are writhing. Larvae as large as our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! are busy gnawing at the petrified secretions that hold the chamber together. While much smaller in size, these are almost certainly juvenile versions of the worms we saw gliding on the surface.\n\nThis must be a nursery of sorts.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:577`

## astral_rift.1820.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1820.a&lang=en)

### 中文

活捉一些标本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

Capture a live specimen.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:578`

## astral_rift.1820.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1820.b&lang=en)

### 中文

总感觉再不走运气就要用完了。赶紧返航。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

We've pushed our luck far enough. Return at once.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:579`

## astral_rift.1820.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1820.b.evopred&lang=en)

取一些样本就回来。

### English

Gather the samples and return.

## astral_rift.1825.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1825.name&lang=en)

### 中文

脱出

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

Pulled Free

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:582`

## astral_rift.1825.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1825.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的锚链可真是生命线啊。\n\n就像系鱼线上的铒一样，我们被从蠕虫的消化道中被拖了出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

Our anchor cable has proved to be a lifeline.\n\nLike bait on a fishing line, we have been pulled free from the gullet of the rockworm.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:583`

## astral_rift.1830.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1830.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被消化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

Digested

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:585`

## astral_rift.1830.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1830.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!上的[explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural]失去了联系。\n\n在回收时，我们拉回的缆绳末端只有半个被消化的金属船壳。\n\n我们借此机会对这些附着在缆绳上的消化液进行了性质研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

We have lost contact with the [explorer.GetTeamMemberPlural] aboard the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nUpon retrieval, we pulled back the wire and at its end was only a partially dissolved hunk of metal.\n\nWe are taking this opportunity to study the unique properties of the digestive fluids coating line.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:586`

## astral_rift.1835.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1835.name&lang=en)

### 中文

开膛破肚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

Rupture

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:588`

## astral_rift.1835.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1835.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

爆破作业成功了。\n\n蠕虫的肉体在我们的下面画作了一滩破碎的岩质肉和内脏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

The explosion was a success.\n\nThe worm's carcass fell below us in a torn heap of rocky flesh and viscera.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:589`

## astral_rift.1835.desc.recover

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1835.desc.recover&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1835.desc.common$\n\n我们发现自己身处于一个岩室内，我们发现了一堆巨大的矿化茧半浸在水里。我们可以取回大量的卵簇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

$astral_rift.1835.desc.common$\n\nWe found ourselves in a chamber containing a clutch of large, mineralized cocoons half-submerged in liquid. We were able to recover a significant amount of these egg clusters.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:590`

## astral_rift.1835.desc.capture

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1835.desc.capture&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1835.desc.common$\n\n脱困之后，我们得以捕获一些幼虫，准备穿过裂隙带回来。\n\n我们应该可以把这些强韧的小东西带回到我们想要的行星上，然后训练它们为我们挖掘矿道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

$astral_rift.1835.desc.common$\n\nFree from all restrictions, we have been able to cage a few of the larvae for transport back through the rift.\n\nWe should be able to transport these durable spawn to a planet of our choosing. Perhaps the younglings can be trained to dig mining tunnels for us.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:591`

## astral_rift.1835.desc.harvest

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1835.desc.harvest&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.1835.desc.common$\n\n脱轨以后，我们得以从这个岩室的水塘里带回大量的卵簇。\n\n我们用晶体外壳装着，把这些强韧的小东西带回到我们想要的行星上，然后训练它们为我们挖掘矿道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

$astral_rift.1835.desc.common$\n\nFree from all restrictions, we have been able to recover a significant amount of egg clusters from the pools inside these chambers.\n\nEncased in crystalline shells, we should be able to transport these durable spawn to a planet of our choosing. Perhaps the younglings can be trained to dig mining tunnels for us.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:592`

## astral_rift.1835.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1835.a&lang=en)

### 中文

听起来怎么有点危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Sounds risky.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:593`

## astral_rift.1835.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1835.b.evopred&lang=en)

先敲碎外壳然后回来。

### English

First crack the shells. Then return.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:594`

## astral_rift.1890.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1890.name&lang=en)

### 中文

兽口脱险

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

From the Belly of the Beast

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:596`

## astral_rift.1890.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1890.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]从星界裂隙另一头回来了，[From.GetHeShe]显得疲惫不堪。\n\n因为在岩蠹的腹中待了许多年，[From.GetHisHer]的身体功能变得极其有限。不论如何，我们都还是让[From.GetHimHer]回到了我们的怀抱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

[From.GetName] [From.GetHasHave] returned from beyond the Astral Rift, but [From.GetHeShe] [From.GetIsAre] the worse for wear.\n\nHaving spent a year inside the belly of a Rockworm, [From.GetHisHer] physical capabilities [From.GetHasHave] become extremely limited. Nonetheless, we have taken [From.GetHimHer] back into our fold.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:597`

## astral_rift.1890.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1890.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那……还是挺幸运的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

How... fortunate?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:598`

## leader_trait_obelisk_curse_tt

### 中文

只要这位领袖还是内阁官员，他们每年就要向黑方尖碑献祭一个人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

As long as this leader is a councilor, they will sacrifice one population to the black obelisk every year.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:608`

## leader_trait_obelisk_curse_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_obelisk_curse_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖是黑色方尖碑的仆人，他要求我们恢复被我们破坏的平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

A servant of the black obelisk, this leader claims to seek to restore the balance we have disrupted.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:609`

## astral_rift_volcano

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift_volcano&lang=en)

火山位面

### English

Volcanic Plane

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:611`

## astral_rift.1900.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1900.name&lang=en)

### 中文

炽热地景

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

Fiery Landscape

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:613`

## astral_rift.1900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

温度难以置信地高。要不是提前准备好的隔热护盾，我们可能就已经葬身烈火了。\n\n远处，有一座黑色的方尖碑静静耸立着。一系列人系开凿的河道汇聚到这座巨大的纪念碑上，把岩石地貌整齐地分割开来。\n\n天空静静地漂浮中发光的球状生物。它们的卷须从精致的身躯上垂下。从这个距离我们还看不出他们是否怀有敌意，但至少在目前，他们似乎没有注意到我们。\n\n方尖碑似乎是最需要的调查对象，但许多漂浮的生物飞得很近，令人不安。也许在离得太近之前观察一下周围的环境会更安全。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Temperatures here are incredibly high. If not for the precautionary heat shields we had applied beforehand, we'd already be incinerated.\n\nLooming in the distance is a black obelisk. A series of artificial channels converge on this vast monument, neatly carving up the rocky landscape.\n\nGlowing, orb-like creatures float quietly in the sky. Bioluminescent tendrils drape from their delicate forms. From this distance we cannot tell if they are hostile or not, but at least for the moment they do not seem to have noticed us.\n\nThe obelisk seems like the most pressing thing to investigate, but many of the floating organisms are flying uncomfortably close. Perhaps it would be safer to observe our surroundings before getting too close.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:614`

## astral_rift.1905.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1905.name&lang=en)

### 中文

追猎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Pursued

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:618`

## astral_rift.1905.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1905.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个鬼地方可比我们一开始想的危险多了。\n\n这里的墙上排列着几间小隔间。里面充满了各种各样的生物体。一看到我们，它们就摇着栅栏，用各种不知名的语言哀号。不时地，我们看到球状的漂浮生物进入这些隔间，用生物电击制服囚犯。\n\n不幸的是，其中一个浮动球体对我们产生了兴趣，并且正在接近我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!。即使我们把引擎开到最大，它也在慢慢追上我们。它的尾迹中拖着长长的卷须，字面上地一路火花带闪电。\n\n在我们下面，一条巨大的熔岩河淹没了黑曜岩地貌。我们准备的隔热层应该能让我们在熔岩里存活一段时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

There is more danger here than we initially thought.\n\nSmall chambers line the walls of this place. They are packed with a wide variety of living organisms. At the sight of us, they are rattling the bars that contain them and wailing in various unknown languages. From time to time, we witness the bulbous, floating creatures enter these cavities and subdue the prisoners with a bio-electric shock.\n\nUnfortunately, one of these floating orbs has taken interest in us and is approaching our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!. Even with our engines at their maximum, it is slowly gaining on us. Its long tendrils trail in its wake, aglow with a heavy electric charge.\n\nBelow us, a vast river of lava floods the obsidian landscape. The heat shielding we have prepared should allow us to survive for a short time under its surface.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:619`

## astral_rift.1905.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1905.a&lang=en)

### 中文

拉动紧急锚链。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Pull the emergency anchor.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:620`

## astral_rift.1905.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1905.b&lang=en)

躲到岩浆里。

### English

Hide in the lava.

## astral_rift.1910.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1910.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被捕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

Captured

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:623`

## astral_rift.1910.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1910.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能躲开探测。在飞向方尖碑的过程中，一小群怪异的漂浮生物很快就追上了我们。\n\n我们现在被他们发光的卷须困住，强行地被拖动着。与之尝试沟通毫无成效。\n\n他们顽固地把我们拖向一个开凿在附近墙体中的笼子里。铁链和栅栏间打开了一条我们的通路。一路上，许多不同形式的感性生命都被关在了类似的隔间里。\n\n这些生物的触须紧紧地抓住了我们的船。然而，通过给我们的引擎增加一剂星界物质，可以产生一股爆发力，帮助我们逃脱它们的控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Our attempts to avoid detection were unsuccessful. As we flew toward the obelisk, a small flock of the strange floating creatures were quick to pursue us.\n\nBound now by their glowing tendrils, we are being towed along forcefully. Communications have proven fruitless.\n\nThey are steadily carrying us toward a cage embedded in the nearby wall. Chains and bars are being pulled aside, making way for our passage. Many diverse forms of sentient life occupy similar enclosures along the way.\n\nThe creatures' tendrils are grasped tightly around our vessel. However, by augmenting our engines with a dose of astral material, we can provide a burst of propulsion that should help us evade their grasp.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:624`

## astral_rift.1910.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1910.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试逃脱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

Attempt to escape.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:625`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1910.b&lang=en)

这太危险了。拉动紧急锚链。

### English

This is dangerous. Pull the emergency anchor.

## astral_rift.1915.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1915.name&lang=en)

### 中文

黑色方尖碑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

The Black Obelisk

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:628`

## astral_rift.1915.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1915.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们悄悄地前进，来到了巨大的方尖碑附近。\n\n黑曜石般的黑色，一直延伸到这个火山空间的天花板。要说它很古老，那它的外表也太无暇了。最早我们认为是装饰的东西其实是某种图案。虽然暂时难以辨认，但熔化的符文在它的表面以一种精心安排的方式旋转。如果有足够的时间，是有可能翻译出来的。\n\n我们的目标跟踪电脑检测到，在这片区域巡逻的生物离这个物体越近时移动得越快。我们推断出，不管这个结构是什么，这些生物都以某种方式与它联系在一起。用压缩气体爆轰摧毁应该会抑制它们的功能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Propelling ourselves quietly along, we have arrived in the close proximity of the massive obelisk.\n\nObsidian black, it stretches to the ceiling of this volcanic chamber. If it is ancient, it is unblemished. What first appeared as decoration has now been revealed itself as a pattern. Yet indecipherable, molten runes swirl along its surface in a deliberate arrangement. Translation would be possible, given enough time.\n\nOur target tracking computers have detected that the creatures patrolling this area move faster the closer they are to this object. We have extrapolated that whatever this structure is, these beings are tethered to it in some way. Destroying it with a charge of compressed gas should inhibit their abilities to function.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:629`

## astral_rift.1915.desc.lava_hide

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1915.desc.lava_hide&lang=en)

### 中文

为了躲避追踪，我们把自己浸没到了下方的熔岩河中。\n\n我们作为预防措施施加的保护膜可以使我们在底下存活足够的时间，游出一段距离，躲避不被漂浮的生物的注意。\n\n$astral_rift.1915.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

To evade notice, we have dipped ourselves into the river of molten lava below us.\n\nThe protective coating we had applied as a precaution allowed us to survive long enough to emerge some distance away, hidden from the attention of the floating creatures.\n\n$astral_rift.1915.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:630`

## astral_rift.1915.desc.lava_escape

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1915.desc.lava_escape&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮生物对我们的行为猝不及防。\n\n引擎的爆发力让我们挣脱了他们的卷须，把我们快速地推向下方的熔岩河。显然，他们还不适应猎物会做出这种反应。\n\n我们作为预防措施施加的保护膜可以使我们在底下存活足够的时间，游出一段距离，躲避不被漂浮的生物的注意。\n\n$astral_rift.1915.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

The floating creatures were not prepared for our sudden maneuver.\n\nA burst of our engines pulled us free of their tendrils, propelling us quickly into the river of molten lava below. Clearly, they are not accustomed to their prey behaving in such a way.\n\nThe protective coating we had applied as a precaution allowed us to survive long enough to emerge some distance away, hidden from their attention.\n\n$astral_rift.1915.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:631`

## astral_rift.1915.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1915.a&lang=en)

解码符文。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

Decipher the runes.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:632`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1915.b&lang=en)

摧毁方尖碑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Destroy the obelisk.

## astral_rift.1920.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1920.name&lang=en)

### 中文

取回

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Retrieval

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:635`

## astral_rift.1920.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1920.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们拉回了锚链，从这个危险的地方逃离。\n\n许多漂浮生物在我们拉回§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的时候一并被拖着走，甚至有一只还被拉回到了我们的维度里，然后马上降解成了一些纯的星界物质。\n\n我们取走了它们的残骸以进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

We retrieved the anchor cable and escaped from this dangerous landscape.\n\nSeveral of the floating creatures latched on to our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! as we were pulled away. One of them even came back with us into our dimension, and promptly disintegrated into pure astral material.\n\nWe are harvesting its remains for further study.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:636`

## astral_rift.1925.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1925.name&lang=en)

### 中文

名单

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Names

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:638`

## astral_rift.1925.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1925.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一份名单\n\n方尖碑上刻满了数以兆计的的名字，以一种复杂的数学模式排列着，我们一边观察它就一边不断变化着。\n\n方尖碑的各个面都有独特的排列方式，使得每个名字都能精确地在沿墙的凹室中显示出来。\n\n用探针触摸任何一个名字都会导致某个相应的房间被危险的能量照亮，并发出可怜的哀号。\n\n不幸的是，在这样做的时候，我们再次吸引了漂浮生物的注意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

It is a list of names.\n\nTrillions and trillions of names cover this obelisk. Arranged in a complicated mathematical pattern, they shift and change as we observe them.\n\nThe facets of the obelisk are uniquely aligned, allowing each name to be precisely true to one of the alcoves along the wall.\n\nTouching any one name with a probe results in a corresponding chamber lighting up with dangerous amounts of energy and a pitiful wailing.\n\nUnfortunately, upon doing this we have attracted the attention of the floating creatures once more.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:639`

## astral_rift.1925.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1925.a&lang=en)

### 中文

够了。拉动应急锚链。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

Enough. Pull the emergency anchor.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:640`

## astral_rift.1925.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1925.b&lang=en)

摧毁个恶劣的装置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

Destroy this infernal device.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:641`

## astral_rift.1930.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1930.name&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎的方尖碑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Shattered Obelisk

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:643`

## astral_rift.1930.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1930.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们策略性地沿着黑曜石塔的接缝放置的炸药把它完全炸成了两半。\n\n方尖碑的上半部分渐渐滑落，黑暗笼罩了整个洞穴。我们之前注意到的漂浮生物失去了光亮，伴随着沉重的而潮湿的砰砰声落在地上。\n\n墙壁两旁的隔间的门被一一打开。无数成群的生物从这些洞里涌进最近的隧道系统。我们不知道这些隧道通向哪里，但至少它们现在自由了。\n\n方尖碑的破碎材料具有独特而有趣的特性。我们已尽可能多地把它带到船上作进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

The charges we strategically placed along the seams of the obsidian tower have sheared it completely in half.\n\nAs the upper portion of the obelisk slid away, a severe darkness filled the cavern. The floating creatures we noticed earlier lost their luminosity and fell to the ground with heavy, wet thumps.\n\nThe gates containing the chambers lining the walls swung open. Countless hordes of creatures poured forth from these holes into the closest tunnel systems. We have no idea where these tunnels lead, but at least they are now free.\n\nThe shattered material of the obelisk possesses unique and interesting properties. We have taken as much of it aboard as we can for further study.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:644`

## astral_rift.1935.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1935.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

Failure

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:646`

## astral_rift.1935.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1935.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与[Leader.GetName]失去了联系。\n\n在取回§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!时，我们发现其中充满了烟雾和灰烬。找不到[Explorer.GetCrew]的尸体。\n\n看来他们永远地迷失在了裂隙另一头了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

We have lost contact with [Leader.GetName].\n\nUpon retrieval of the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!, we discovered it filled with smoke and ash. The bodies of the [Explorer.GetCrew] were not recovered.\n\nIt is likely they are lost forever beyond the rift.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:647`

## astral_rift.1940.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1940.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不祥访客

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

Ominous Visitor

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:649`

## astral_rift.1940.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1940.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个熔融生物要求见我们。他自称是我们六年前炸毁的那座黑色方尖碑的所有者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

A molten being is requesting audience with us. They claim to be the owners of the black obelisk we destroyed exactly six years ago.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:650`

## astral_rift.1941.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1941.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们对自己的所作所为一无所知。\n\n你错误地释放了我们的囚犯。如果平衡得不到恢复，将会产生严重的后果。\n\n接下来的六年，你们每年都要从你们的殖民地提供囚犯代为关押。我们将以公平的交易作为回报。\n\n如果你拒绝，我们很乐意无所不用其极地折磨你的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

You know not what you have done.\n\nYou have mistakenly freed our wards. Balance must be restored, or there will be great consequences.\n\nEach year, for six years, you will offer us replacement prisoners from your colonies. We shall offer a fair trade in return.\n\nShould you decline, we shall happily torment your people until the suffering is complete.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:652`

## astral_rift.1941.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1941.a&lang=en)

### 中文

行。那就恢复平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

Very well. Restore the Balance.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:653`

## astral_rift.1941.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1941.b&lang=en)

### 中文

滚蛋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

Be gone.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:654`

## astral_rift.1950.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1950.name&lang=en)

## astral_rift.1950.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1950.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.GetName]从$astral_rift_volcano$星界裂隙回来了……但和我们对他们的记忆不太有一样。\n\n同位素采样表明[from.GetHisHer]的年龄已经超过了600岁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

[from.GetName] [from.GetHasHave] returned from the $astral_rift_volcano$ Astral Rift... but not as we remember them.\n\nCarbon-samples indicate [from.GetHisHer] age is now over 600 years old.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:657`

## astral_rift.1955.name

### 中文

牺牲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Sacrifice

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:660`

## astral_rift.1955.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1955.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事态向令人不安的方向发展了。\n\n[from.GetName]秘密地在把我们的人口通过$astral_rift_volcano$传送走。\n\n他们称这是在进行“恢复平衡”的努力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

We have discovered a disturbing development.\n\n[from.GetName] has been secretly sending members of our population back through the $astral_rift_volcano$ Astral Rift.\n\nThey claim it is being done in an effort to "restore the balance."

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:661`

## MESSAGE_RESTORING_THE_BALANCE_SACRIFICE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_RESTORING_THE_BALANCE_SACRIFICE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[event_target:sacrificed_pop_planet.GetName]的人口被送进了$astral_rift_volcano$星界裂隙里，以恢复被我们干扰的平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Population from [event_target:sacrificed_pop_planet.GetName] has been sent back into the $astral_rift_volcano$ Astral Rift, in an effort to restore the balance we have disrupted.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_1_l_english.yml:666`
